# Decennium Descent — Expedition Director Book

Read the [Expedition Player Book](EXPEDITION_PLAYER_BOOK.md) for the complete player procedures. This companion holds all Director procedures, worked chapter cases, record contracts and complete integrated examples. Exact GPT review remains pending.

Source faction dossiers, full class envelopes, original proposals and conflict history remain in the [complete Director data](../../../data/director/expedition_candidate/complete_candidate.json). They are preserved as source evidence, not public player knowledge.

1. [Charters, time and provisions](#director-chapter-1)
2. [Routes, extraction and retreat](#director-chapter-2)

## Explicit review issue

FLOW-C01 adds two supported feeding intervals to the preserved original example. Net elapsed time increases by 59 minutes. Review the entire revised chronology, including rest, light, provisions, faction work and provider schedules. The original outline is retained in completeComponents.integrated_flow.originalGPTIntegratedSequence.

<a id="director-chapter-1"></a>
## 1. Charters, time and provisions

### FL-01 — The rules used on an expedition

This module is an overlay beside the preserved original Loop v1.2. Keep the reviewed roll/damage/Wound/death, action/stance, condition/control, Strain/rest, body/equipment, creature/device lifecycle, language, recipe and acquired class/spell rules controlling within their own scope. Do not add generic team, critical-output or restoration rewards to an exact procedure.

The existing LAX, NWX, LRX and TEX fulfillments and 687 internal/433 public recipe scopes remain unchanged. The 136 reviewed standalone low-rank procedures require their complete authored representation and actual class access; do not invent a conversion into a different runtime. Blink remains null and unavailable. Pending cards stay pending; this module changes no class, feat or spell approval. Ordinary spell totals remain 412 reviewed and 436 pending.

### FL-02 — Members, teams and offices

Check concurrent roster membership by actual role, not a convenient label. Track actual encounter participants separately from roster membership, with each companion's source caps. Holding two offices never creates two workers. A remote scout cannot transmit knowledge or lend a class/item benefit without actual communication and the relevant rule.

Resolve parallel teams chronologically. If one team's narrated activity reaches hour 8, resolve the other teams' events before hour 8 before advancing beyond it. Their work does not add another eight hours to the world.

The original downloaded EC-01 contract mistakenly includes Lorekeeper and omits two source offices. GPT's final EX58-EDIT-01 confirms the five source offices above and corrects exactly the JSON field and its Markdown table cell. Both current derived contracts now use the exact replacement, with matching measured hashes. The original package and its 2,316-check report remain unchanged. No other EX58 decision or office duty changes.

### FL-03 — One clock, work and rest

Record nonnegative integer seconds from one campaign epoch. Watch w is [epoch + 28,800w, epoch + 28,800(w + 1)); a day is 86,400 seconds. Preserve Opening 2,400 hours and Horn 1,200 hours as literal scheduling receipts only. The Opening start is one recorded epoch event, not a fresh clock for each entry. This module supplies no post-Opening access or next-Cycle renewal and interprets no cosmology.

Measure actual work to the second and display minutes/hours. Generic interrupted progress remains only while physically feasible; actual loss can undo it. Travel's nominal tranche is distinct from its adjusted elapsed duration. No general Watch label changes an exact procedure's active-work requirements or crafting's own two-Watch productive ceiling.

At equal timestamps, use this order outside initiative:

1. Finish actual transfers/consumption and protection endings under their precise endpoint rules.
2. Evaluate due food/water support and blocks.
3. Complete qualifying rest/treatment; a safe Rest can clear debt here.
4. Process a still-unpaid debt deadline.
5. Settle completed activity/travel.
6. Coalesce extraction checkpoints.
7. Apply once-per-event/Watch Pressure and its bounded complication batch.
8. Run due faction/checkpoint reporting.

Combat's own expiration, death, start and periodic ordering controls inside initiative. Do not backdate supplies, protection, rest or rewards after learning an outcome. Stable recovery retains its original absolute deadline and does not replay at a Watch boundary. Later route, Pressure and faction modules supply their full procedures; this partial module does not substitute for them.

### FL-04 — Supply and daily support

Model one canonical recipient/day settlement with unique consumption and shortfall events. Demand is proportional to actual seconds inside each interval; retain exact rational amounts rather than rounding at every transfer. With a changed dietary coefficient, integrate each coefficient over its real interval. Outside-expedition support is separate actual provisioning, not an exemption.

A missed necessary logistics task retains its actual exposure/visibility consequence and may leave that recipient unsupported. It does not universally cause hunger or automatically add another Strain. The once-daily shortfall includes all missing components. Later failure retains or creates its real event without refunding past consumption. Death/collapse still follows the reviewed Strain rule.

Food, water, fuel, containers and sundries remain distinct physical lots with origin, custody, mass, volume and clocks. Donations transfer stock; they do not replenish the donor's consumed history. A full procedural basket and nutrition credits must point to the same actual goods and use events. No virtual Supply token exists alongside the contents. Keep every no-resource, no-sale or similar source restriction through transfers and repacking.

### FL-05 — Buying, packing and keeping provisions

The sundry price/mass, water and meal quantity mappings, ordinary ration expiry, handling minute and oil-flask tare split are explicit NEW EX58 choices beside the original source fields. Do not rewrite printed source values or turn absent fields into historical data. The low-tier basket arithmetic is 50 + 1 + 10 + 2 = 63 cp and 2 + 8 + 1 + 0.25 = 11.25 lb. Initial skins add 40 cp and 2 lb. A partial ordinary flask weighs 0.25 + 0.75 × remaining pints. A broken or burned vessel follows its actual loss without producing an intact duplicate.

Keep source-qualified magic finite:

- Purify Food and Drink removes only its reviewed nonmagical contaminants from one real separated food batch of at most 5 lb OR at most one gallon of drink. It creates no amount and does not reverse spoilage.
- Conjure Water retains its at-most-one-gallon output, one active batch, one-hour maximum, recall on recast and once-per-recipient/24-hour hydration. Credit only actual consumption before its endpoint. It creates no saleable stored stock, reagent or whole Supply.
- Shape Water moves actual eligible water. Water Breathing changes respiration, not hydration, swimming Speed or cargo capacity.
- Goodberry and all other sources keep their exact recipient, output, access, cost and lifetime. There is no general conversion from a food-spell name into Supply.

Holy water, oil or a named marrow is not edible by its label. A full medical kit and proper recipe material remain separately required. Actual support equipment may be shared only within its real functional limits. This comparison does not activate a pending spell or give a character unacquired access.

### FL-06 — Foraging a finite place

Before the attempt, record the opportunity's site/route-segment ID, physical bounds, initial food/water/material vector, prior claims, ordinary S allowance and disjoint critical reserve. The sustainable physical limit is separate from S: S is the ordinary recoverable allowance, and the critical extra is at most ceil(S/2) from the pre-existing reserve. Do not infer matter from a route rating.

Simultaneous and serial claimants share the same unresolved claim. Any subdivisions preserve the parent's aggregate ordinary and extra allowances. Later genuine growth, delivery, replenishment or a newly accessible disjoint area can establish a new opportunity with its own physical quantity and lineage; a new map label or a Director boolean alone cannot.

Do not duplicate fishing, carcass, mining or salvage claims. A critical failure supplies no invented poison, property or nonexistent victim. Keep the once-per-cause Pressure event, rather than adding another because the same failure appears in a team summary.

### FL-07 — Building and using a camp

Maintain one build ledger per site. Teams can work on separate necessary portions; they cannot reroll a finished step for additional marks or fixtures. Record installed and surviving materials, marks, sleeping places, guards, alarms, lighting, care/work surfaces, closures and current hazards. Rest or Wound-treatment safety depends on those actual conditions. Camp tiers do not create every trade's workshop.

The supply-line record names endpoints, provider, carrier bodies/crew, trip capacity, paid charges, departure/arrival times, risk and missed deliveries. A depot label does not create a delivery.

A Severe complication can degrade exactly one achieved tier or require evacuation only when its actual damage/discovery supports that consequence. Preserve surviving materials and prior marks. Restore affected functionality through one newly necessary build task and actual lost-material replacement. Exposure removes concealment/warning predicates only as actually compromised. If storage capacity falls, relocate the excess; do not delete cargo. No replacement grants a duplicate intact item or resets a reward claim.

### Worked chapter cases

#### EX01-01

**Situation:** Two teams each work from campaign hour 0 to hour 8.

**Expected result:** The world reaches hour 8, not hour 16. Each actual worker has one compatible interval; team labels create no extra hours.

#### EX01-02

**Situation:** A generic task has 300 active minutes, then resumes for 180 feasible active minutes in a later Watch.

**Expected result:** It reaches one 480-minute completion check. The earlier interruption gave no partial completion reward. An exact uninterrupted recipe or training source may behave differently.

#### EX01-03

**Situation:** An unpaid Rest Debt deadline and the completion of a qualifying safe eight-hour Rest are both at campaign second 144000.

**Expected result:** Complete the Rest and clear debt before processing the still-unpaid-debt step. That debt causes no deadline Strain. Long-Rest reward eligibility remains a separate clock.

#### EX01-04

**Situation:** A Medium recipient has no food and no potable water at the same daily settlement.

**Expected result:** One shortfall event gives 1 expedition Strain total and the unsupported-day block. It does not give one Strain for each missing component.

#### EX01-05

**Situation:** A recipient spends half a canonical day with the Medium coefficient and half with the Large coefficient.

**Expected result:** That day requires 3 lb food and 12 pints water. Goods already consumed remain consumed. No form-change refund or new daily window appears.

#### EX01-06

**Situation:** Four people use one suitable actual lamp for six hours, with its light genuinely sufficient for all four.

**Expected result:** Spend one pint of lamp oil once. Do not charge four pints merely because four support records refer to that lamp.

#### EX01-07

**Situation:** Buy a standard low-tier basket and two new empty waterskins at the stated reference prices.

**Expected result:** Pay 103 cp and carry 13.25 lb after packing. The eight pints of water are counted once, plus two pounds of skin tare.

#### EX01-08

**Situation:** The ordinary new one-pint oil-flask profile has half a pint of oil remaining.

**Expected result:** It weighs 0.25 + 0.75 × 0.5 = 0.625 lb. The same intact flask remains; no duplicate empty flask is generated.

#### EX01-09

**Situation:** A Sustenance-3 opportunity contains its ordinary allocation and a real extra reserve equivalent to one unit. The check is a Critical Success.

**Expected result:** The formula permits at most two extra units, but the reserve permits only one. Recover at most four actual provision-equivalent units with their real component vector, not five invented baskets.

#### EX01-10

**Situation:** Two teams rename a depleted site and visit during the next Watch without actual replenishment.

**Expected result:** No new stock or allowance exists. Their changed labels and timing cannot support a second award.

#### EX01-11

**Situation:** An otherwise qualifying camp reaches Forward Base with all baseline new fixture packs.

**Expected result:** It has 8 required marks and 150 Load storage. The baseline packs total 870 lb/187 gp; prior capacities do not add to the new capacity. Shelter and real supply-line costs remain separate.

#### EX01-12

**Situation:** A Quartermaster holds another office and reads a pending spell name in a supplies comparison.

**Expected result:** Neither the second office nor the comparison grants an extra work interval, spell acquisition, class access or pending-option approval.

<a id="director-chapter-2"></a>
## 2. Routes, extraction and retreat

### FL-08 — Pressure follows the expedition’s real exposure

Maintain one parent Expedition, shared clock and stable physical cause/Watch IDs. Separated groups can have different real exposure. Splitting grants neither a new Pressure pool nor a reduction; on merging use the greatest current Pressure, retain all causes/deadlines and apply the destination floor. An unrelated Expedition does not erase shared pursuit or cargo histories.

Keep continuing Watch causes separate from discrete Attention, forced-march, retreat and extraction-surcharge events. Forced march adds its +2 once; successful retreat its +1 once. A second textual description is not a second charge. Each allowed reduction has a unique source; blocked reduction below the floor is logged, not stored for later. Base Risk is 1–5, with the explicit new completion allowing an already Secured Risk-0 profile; do not infer zero risk otherwise.

A Watch is active when it contains actual expedition travel, work or exposure-bearing activity. Pressure 6 is evaluated at the due checkpoint after the applicable signed batch, not on every intermediate arithmetic value. Immediate real hazards keep their own timestamps.

For partial-Watch safe exit, close that actual exposure's Watch/cause membership once. A later genuinely new exposure uses its real remaining interval and causes, without resetting earlier ones. A later descent retains relevant unresolved Pressure/pursuit history and the new route floor; only an actual qualifying reduction changes it.

### FL-09 — Complications have a declared cause and a finite response

Select a finite compatible entry with its actual source before committing the check; replace an impossible entry before resolution, never after a successful response to defeat it. Modified/rerolled equality is the explicit replacement for the original impossible phrase “exceeds by exactly 0.” An untouched 3 at Pressure 3 is Standard; a legal −1 taking 4 to 3 is Minor; a final triggering natural 1 stays Severe.

Give each root Watch/checkpoint incident one causal ID. At one timestamp, merge duplicate requests for the same group and physical cause, keeping the highest severity and all reasons. Unrelated real incidents remain distinct.

A Severe complication during extraction can request one subsequent Extraction Test for its changed convoy, recorded as secondary to that root. A complication caused by this secondary test cannot request another test in the same causal batch. The finite route positions, root events and one permitted secondary per root bound the queue. Delays can reach genuinely later Watch/day events on the shared clock; those are new due events, not recursion at the old timestamp.

### FL-10 — Route travel and mapping

Keep exact rational fractional Progress when shorter intervals are needed to locate crossed checkpoints; splitting a ledger creates no additional completion check or base award. Record position-to-Progress bindings and actual obstacles. Zero Length is a location record, not repeatable travel/reward.

Travel duration changes the same actual traverse; do not also award independent mileage or work-time credit. Genuine environment/quality/pace factors apply once, and the Strain-5 exception never halves again at a new Watch. Actual pauses add their real elapsed time. Section TR-13's full transport procedure still controls specific method changes and transfers.

Apply a route-quality encounter adjustment once in the final difficulty, only when that threat is not already represented. Route-quality cargo-loss rows are declared stakes under the failure-response procedure; they do not create a second automatic loss roll. Mapping marks and route-quality changes require their own real evidence; neither is inferred from the other.

### FL-11 — Forced march and interrupted travel

Use the current Core fixed-DC task degrees and natural shifts, not attack or saving-throw rules. Ordinary route tier DCs retained from the source are I 12, II 13, III 15, IV 16, V 18 and VI 19; Severe adds 6. These numbers do not certify Tier VI content readiness.

Count the forced-march +2 once by its cause ID, even when a Watch summary also describes the march. Its extra basket commitment is distinct from an abstract daily Supply debit; actual components, consumers and remaining amounts are tracked under part 1. Retain costs, disturbances and partial progress across legitimate interruptions, with no stock refund for physically consumed material. Do not award another Watch just for reaching a segment endpoint.

### FL-12 — Starting extraction and admitting cargo

Record the real episode, return destination, cargo lineage and surcharge high-water mark. Room re-entry, allied transfers and a new ID do not reset it. A genuinely concluded return followed by a new descent can begin a new episode, while unresolved original cargo retains condition, clocks and custody.

Reference each securing operation to actual harness/container/care/containment rules. Cargo priority chooses among real eligible stakes; it cannot create nonexistent victims or override legal status. Keep carrier admission separate from extraction risk: Class affects handling DC, not physical pound/Load limits or access.

### FL-13 — Extraction checkpoints and checks

Use actual route-position IDs, not only displayed Progress numbers. Resolve each finite routine checkpoint at most once. Extra Progress queues newly crossed positions in order after the current test; it does not recursively resolve them inside an unfinished result. Exit is not passed until its required final test is resolved. No new room/route IDs can be invented to manufacture critical Progress.

A real Severe complication that changes convoy state can request one test at a previously checked position under the root/secondary limit. Retain all coalesced reasons, distinct actual operations, and pending queue state.

Calculate Burden from actual occupied transport and its correct thresholds; duplicating a body/container record cannot change it. Verification work is real labor, not passive carriage, a free off-turn Action or a ledger update. The DC cap groups only Class, Attention, Burden and unsecured dangerous lots; pursuit and Secured remain outside it.

### FL-14 — A failed extraction test creates a choice and a real problem

Core route-quality loss rows become declared stake guides, not automatic inventory subtraction in addition to the chosen Loop failure branch: Known threatens one actual Load; Contested 10% of current carried Load; Collapsed 20% or a real reroute; Secured has no automatic routine cargo-loss row. Identify real whole lots or divisible portions, preserving indivisibility and actor safety.

Special Living/Changed or Pact Interdiction rows require their actual defined event/seizure/service permission. An absent profile supplies no invented power. Disabled vehicles and casualties use the complete transport-repair/rescue and physical/life rules.

Coalesce coincident duplicate complication requests by physical cause while retaining independent incidents. The response ledger names actual time, labor, resources, current location and realized damage/loss; an abstract risk flag is not proof of an insured loss or destroyed property.

### FL-15 — Retreat without restarting the encounter

Threat attacks preserve real range, costs, defenses, damage and source callbacks. Abstract chase displacement does not also trigger another tactical opportunity-attack sequence. When exact tactical movement/attack exchange is needed, use Encounter Mode and declare that exchange's single contribution to the round before resolution; do not combine full opposing combat and abstract chase punishment.

An Action committed to the lead check is unavailable for another action. Declared cargo sacrifices consume or relocate actual cargo; gestures do not create class-feature output. Maintain unique increment causes and the generic per-round limits, then apply genuine express source exceptions only within their real scope.

A physically trapped route cannot be bypassed by another check. Present real surrender, rescue, alternative fight or route options under their actual permissions. Carrying a casualty still requires the actual carrier and its movement/Load/body limits.

### FL-16 — Estimate encounters from actual participants

For this provisional estimate, party level is floor(the arithmetic mean of actual participating characters' character levels), minimum 1. An enemy's actual assigned creature level controls; otherwise retain its exact rational CR. Bin floor(enemy level/CR − party level) using this source table:

| Relative level | Threat Points |
|---:|---:|
| −4 or lower | 5 |
| −3 | 10 |
| −2 | 15 |
| −1 | 25 |
| 0 | 40 |
| +1 | 60 |
| +2 | 90 |
| +3 | 135 |
| +4 or higher | 200 |

CR 1/4 against level 1 bins −1 here. CR supplies no unprinted creature ability or imported stat block.

Compute N_eff as actual participating independent character turns plus 0.5 for each actual limited attached-phase offensive companion/device contributing to that scene. Count no passive gear, absent member, support-only decoration or duplicate actor. A full-turn recruited NPC contributes 1 with its proper roster/service status. Keep the real profiles and command limits; a half actor is not a claim of equal power across all classes. Do not produce an encounter budget for zero participants.

Multiply the following four-character budgets by max(0.2, 1 + 0.2 × (N_eff − 4)). This keeps the source 3/4/5/6 participant multipliers 0.8/1/1.2/1.4 without exponential compounding.

| Difficulty | Base budget | Source success target, not observed performance |
|---|---:|---|
| Routine | 50 | 95–100% |
| Standard | 90 | 70–90% |
| Hard | 130 | 40–65% |
| Severe | 180 | 15–35% |
| Catastrophic | 240 | 0–10% |

Compare enemy totals and budgets, then apply each actual objective, hostile terrain, surprise and active hazard adjustment once in the final label. Cargo/hazard normally adds one step; ordinary surprise adds one; a genuinely warranted full unopposed enemy round adds two, without another automatic addition for that same surprise. Route-quality −1/+1 adjustments apply only where the same threat has not already been counted. Escort needs an explicit progress/survival/movement clock; retreat uses its actual Escape/Pursuit clock.

Retain the calibration limitations: level 1–2 Standard encounters should avoid pairs of above-level enemies; levels 5–16 need meaningful objectives/phases rather than arbitrary HP inflation; levels 17–20 need actual party-composition testing. Proxy estimates justify no global class buffs, nerfs or guarantee of survival. Gate Guardian's second Tenacity use still requires its actual source, and old global control-immunity shorthand does not override the current hard-control procedure.

### FL-17 — Transport admission preserves every body and item

The complete candidate must include all 29 original transport rows and their full operating profiles, plus the distinct Core service/auxiliary profiles under TR-01–14. Those complete profiles are still assembled separately from this module. Keep every NEW profile value separate from the untouched source row, including comma placeholders and null/missing mass, speed or capacity. Preserve Player prices, all nine printed mount speeds/carry limits, and original Cart HP 30 as durability only.

Complete profiles support authoring and synthetic adjudication. Actually acquiring and using an asset still requires its ordinary real provider, access, body, stock, crew and permission. Do not treat a completed mechanical profile as evidence those particular world facts exist. No pending mounted feat or companion ability is promoted by this integration.

### FL-18 — Durability, old Integrity records and repair

Preserve historical Integrity without migrating a real save. Only where a source-tagged integer I in {0,1,2,3} is the sole condition evidence for a genuinely identified complete body with newly established maximum structural HP H, derive conservative initial HP = floor(H × I / 3). Keep the original raw I and interpretation source. I = 0 maps to 0; I = 3 maps to H only if the source actually asserts intact sound condition and no later damage evidence exists.

Credible current HP/damage controls instead; never raise it from old Integrity. Conflicting chronology remains unresolved until an actual correction event. Unknown, unversioned or malformed values remain raw and nonoperative. Do not inverse-write a derived interpretation into a historical save.

Optional display labels are Broken 0 at HP 0, Damaged 1 at 0 < HP ≤ H/3, Worn 2 above that but below H, and Sound 3 at H. These labels are display only, not another operative condition track. Unmodeled route Integrity may remain condition prose without granting a repair or hauling permission. Camp tier, route security and supply-line function keep their own event/build records.

Use the exact current item's repair procedure. Existing grade/size pricing precedes the material calculation. Service repair is a separate genuine quote under the equipment/service rules; do not add self-repair material cost to an inclusive service bill. Preserve all source fields and actual surviving material. This interpretation does not read or modify actual character/campaign saves.

### Worked chapter cases

#### EX02-01

**Situation:** Two groups with Pressure 2 and 4 merge on a Risk-3 route.

**Expected result:** Use Pressure 4 with both histories. The merge does not produce a fresh pool or lower Pressure to the route floor.

#### EX02-02

**Situation:** One Attention lot stays exposed through three Watches and changes containers.

**Expected result:** Its original continuous exposure causes one discrete increase. The same exposed state can still contribute the single Extraction DC modifier; repacking does not renew the cause.

#### EX02-03

**Situation:** A group reaches safe Haven at hour 6, before its current Watch has had its routine exposure checkpoint.

**Expected result:** Settle that checkpoint at the last exposed boundary using actual accrued causes. Mark it processed. Do not roll it again at hour 8 or create a route patrol inside Haven.

#### EX02-04

**Situation:** At Pressure 3, the final natural die is 4 and a lawful −1 yields 3.

**Expected result:** Minor. An untouched natural 3 would instead give Standard. The procedure itself grants no −1 or reroll.

#### EX02-05

**Situation:** At Pressure 3, a final natural 1 has a lawful +3 modifier.

**Expected result:** The modified 4 does not trigger. Natural 1 is Severe only after the trigger test succeeds.

#### EX02-06

**Situation:** A Severe root complication changes an extracting convoy; its one secondary Extraction Test also generates a Severe complication.

**Expected result:** Resolve the second complication, but do not generate another secondary test in that same causal batch. A genuinely later due Watch event still has its own timestamp.

#### EX02-07

**Situation:** A fit 3-mph convoy traverses a Contested route with a 60-minute pause.

**Expected result:** Its nominal 480 minutes becomes 600 moving minutes, plus the real 60-minute pause, for 660 elapsed minutes. Do not also award separate mileage or an extra eight-hour Watch.

#### EX02-08

**Situation:** An Unknown traverse is made feasible by its own successful moving reconnaissance, with the permitted Progress bonus selected.

**Expected result:** Use base 1 plus at most one shared reconnaissance +1, then the actual route-capacity penalty if any. A same-tranche mapping improvement does not retroactively grant base 2.

#### EX02-09

**Situation:** Three Medium active participants and one Large participant succeed at a feasible forced march with their extras reserved.

**Expected result:** The extra support commitment is five complete baskets, subject to actual specific diet exceptions and real consumption history. Gain 1 Progress and +2 Pressure once; a critical grants the same march result.

#### EX02-10

**Situation:** An extraction episode starts with highest Class 2 and later acquires a genuinely new Class 6 lot.

**Expected result:** The original surcharge is 1; the high-water value becomes 3, adding only 2. Dropping the Class 6 lot does not refund the surcharge.

#### EX02-11

**Situation:** A Mapped route has Length 7.

**Expected result:** Freeze routine midpoint 3.5 and exit 7. Each uses an actual position. Reaching the midpoint via extra Progress queues it once, not recursively inside the previous result.

#### EX02-12

**Situation:** Tier II DC 13, highest Class 4, exposed Attention, Burden, three unsecured dangerous lots, pursued and Secured.

**Expected result:** The cargo subtotal 9 caps at 7, then +2 and −2 apply outside it: DC 20. A lot with both Volatile and Cursed counts once.

#### EX02-13

**Situation:** A checkpoint requires stationary verification, then its failed test selects the eight-hour delay.

**Expected result:** Count one actual verification minute and 480 delay minutes. The delay gives no stalled-convoy Progress; independent safe rest is possible only if its actual conditions qualify.

#### EX02-14

**Situation:** A reachable Class 3 crate is at risk and no more specific recovery source applies, at Tier II.

**Expected result:** Declare the actual 60-minute task at DC 16 with required rigging/helpers. Failure uses its declared stranded position or hazard; it does not automatically delete the crate or pay insurance.

#### EX02-15

**Situation:** A retreat round would finish at Escape 3 and Pursuit 3 after its declared costs and threat.

**Expected result:** Pursuit reaches the failure threshold, so the retreat fails. This grants the actual next enemy position/opportunity, not automatic death or ownership.

#### EX02-16

**Situation:** The lead rolls a Critical Success while an ally achieves the declared blocking objective.

**Expected result:** The generic Escape award remains 2 from the lead critical, not 3. The ally does not generate another lead check; any express paid feature needs its actual separate permission.

#### EX02-17

**Situation:** Four independent participating characters and one actually contributing limited attached-phase offensive companion face an encounter.

**Expected result:** N_eff is 4.5 and multiplier 1.1. Standard reference budget is 99. This is a provisional numerical estimate, not a runtime or balance result.

#### EX02-18

**Situation:** An enemy has only CR 1/4 and the actual party reference level is 1.

**Expected result:** floor(1/4 − 1) = −1, giving 25 provisional Threat Points. Its actual stat block still determines its abilities.

#### EX02-19

**Situation:** A carrier transports a transformed ally with 20 lb of melded gear.

**Expected result:** Include the ally’s effective body mass and all melded/external gear in external carriage. The gear is not weightless for the carrier, and the separate casualty/gear Loads still apply.

#### EX02-20

**Situation:** A complete cart has only source-qualified Integrity 2 and a newly established maximum of 30 structural HP.

**Expected result:** Derive 20 HP while preserving raw Integrity 2. If credible current damage instead establishes 7 HP, retain 7; the old Integrity cannot heal it.

#### EX02-21

**Situation:** A recoverable ordinary cart has maximum 30 HP and a proper 15-gp baseline.

**Expected result:** Self-repair takes 3 active hours and 3.75 gp of compatible material, then a DC 10 binary-outcome tool task. It does not restore cargo or grant a second cart.

#### EX02-22

**Situation:** The convoy exceeds route Capacity but one individual carrier also exceeds its own hard limit.

**Expected result:** The −1 Progress/+1 Pressure route penalty does not legalize the carrier’s overload. Transfer, drop, split or use a real permitted alternative first.

<a id="director-chapter-3"></a>
## 3. Haven, settlement and advancement

### FL-19 — Arriving at Haven

Record intake arrivals, manifest, restrictions and discrepancies. Preserve completed work when interrupted unless the actual interruption invalidates it; keep paid fees, service time and material age. Unknown cargo cannot be made safe by selecting the fast route.

The governing record supplies appraisal DC even if the tier is not known to the characters. Unsupported higher-tier or exceptional objects require their actual assessment source; do not invent a DC. A skill name supplies no supernatural detection. Critical detail/buyer access must be supported and declared, not universal function, provenance, curse discovery or a guaranteed best market.

Record an appraiser's belief separately from true facts. New evidence or a genuinely different appropriate test can justify another check. A successful title search alone grants no ownership; an enchantment's identification alone grants no complete missing powers. Preserve the source references and completed procedural boundaries rather than resetting the episode when it changes administrative route.

### FL-20 — Choose one valuation method

For a true artifact, preserve the Core formula:

`BaseFunctionValue × GateTierMultiplier × artifact Quality × Provenance × Demand × Legality − actual uncounted costs`

Keep these source quality factors separate from manufacturing/equipment grades:

| Artifact market quality | Source factor |
|---|---:|
| Crude/damaged | 0.25 |
| Standard | 1 |
| Fine | 1.5 |
| Superior | 2.5 |
| Exceptional | 5 |
| Masterwork or named | 10 |

The source also retains no provenance ×0.5, verified Gate Seal ×1.1–1.5, urgent strategic demand ×2–10, and the illegal-but-desired row ×0.5 lawful/×2 black market. These apply only through a legitimate particular profile/offer with actual predicates and lawful disposition; a range gives no automatic maximum. Sentient, cursed or contagious objects need their actual liability assessment. These source labels do not interpret cosmology or create a new market.

The general table supplies no numeric BaseFunctionValue or GateTierMultiplier. An actual artifact record/finite offer must state its missing values; never invent Tier-VII–X formulas or borrow unrelated item prices. Preserve gross, stabilization, transport, tax and liability entries separately, charging only expenses not already counted. Actual seizure, consent and insurance keep their own procedures.

An ordinary lot's NEW fallback reference names its exact quantity/mass, source band and finite identity before pricing. Subdivision allocates the parent's reference exactly; it creates no new catalog commodity or extra total value. Original Loop and Core formulas, including conflicting grade words, stay preserved in the source layer. All 687 reviewed recipe references retain their domain-specific restrictions.

### FL-21 — One settlement ledger

Every actual cost has one event identity. Do not charge an already-paid provision purchase, transport premium, tool or sponsor advance again. Distinguish using existing capital, buying new capital, financing and operating expense explicitly in the episode's accounts rather than hiding them in a single “net” value.

If an artifact net quote already deducted rescue/quarantine or tax, reconstruct its gross and the visible deductions before applying the sponsor formula. Do not subtract the same deduction from net again. A deposit and final balance settle one bill; an insurance claim does not create another sale of missing cargo.

Preserve exact parent-lot value allocations through subdivisions; individually rounded child estimates cannot exceed the parent's historical reference merely by splitting. Real separately agreed transactions keep their legitimate final rounding. Unknown exchange rates and unresolved liabilities remain explicit, not zeroed for convenience.

### FL-22 — Claims, sponsors, taxes and insurance

Preserve actual claims, unmet obligations and each claimant's authority. Emergency-use priority needs a genuinely necessary authorized use, not unrestricted consumption. Establish permitted disposal first, then calculate/remit the transaction's taxes and sponsor base. No appraisal settles title.

Core alternative sponsor offers remain distinct negotiations:

| Offer | Source terms to complete in the actual contract |
|---|---|
| Merchant | 20–35% gross; agreed first-purchase option |
| Guild | 15–25%; actual dues and stated formula/patent rights |
| Noble | 25–50%; particular strategic claims |
| Religious | Actual tithe and relic priority |
| Consortium | 30–60%; actual liens/audit |
| Criminal | 40–70%; actual unlawful risks |

Where a row omits its percentage base or exact tithe, the offer must supply it. Do not import another row's default. These are alternatives to the Loop net-share defaults, not additional shares.

Core also offers separate Life/Return at 5% of expected share, Cargo at 8–20% value, Gate-Delay at 3%, Artifact-Liability at 10%+, and an annual ransom bond. They are not automatic riders or duplicate premiums on the standard Loop policy. Before purchase, every binder needs an exact premium, currency/value basis, term, named beneficiary, finite payout cap, deductible/rate or fixed-benefit formula, covered trigger, evidence, exclusions and notice/due terms. A missing annual fee, payout size or other undefined term needs an ordinary finite offer; the incomplete policy cannot be bought or claimed.

Life/Return keeps its actual named death-and-body-recovery trigger. Delay requires an actual covered closure/Guardian delay, not poor planning. Liability needs its real lawful-sale loss and disclosed risks. Ransom needs actual capture, authorized payee and release terms. Preserve exceptional exclusions as literal source receipts without cosmological interpretation. Notice, accepted claim, entitlement and payment remain different records.

### FL-23 — Standing and earned history

This is an explicit NEW adoption, replacing the older Loop −5…+5 standing/access ladder and the Player's Revered +5 / Respected +3 / Friendly +1 / Neutral 0 / Suspicious −1 / Hostile −3 / Hated −5 automatic social-modifier table in the integrated ordinary procedure. Preserve all three full tables and source versions. The Player numbers were modifiers, not evidence of a saved standing scale. Activating an old record requires its source-version interpretation; neither old table silently stacks with standing-v1.

Preserve the runtime Cult Escapee religious-orders:+1 map as historical data. Only when its source and later earned events establish that it was an erroneous initialization, explicitly replace that initial contribution with −1 and replay the real events. For example, erroneous +1 plus earned +1 becomes corrected −1 plus earned +1 = 0. If origin/history is unknown, retain raw +1 and flag that relationship rather than guessing. Full lossless handling and all 20 background comparisons follow RP-A01–06.

Keep Loop renown as historical/cultural descriptors: surviving Tier I, proven adventurer; reaching Tier II, established professional; profitable Haven return, career-defining success; operating through Tier III, elite expedition member; returning from IV, famous or feared; returning from V, living legend. Whether an NPC knows a deed needs actual information. Sixth-Gate, seasonal and deeper-setting statements stay literal receipts only, granting no faction number, authority or cosmological conclusion.

### FL-24 — Haven work, services, learning and recovery

Keep all 687 recipe creation/research/copying procedures, finite primary bodies/ingredients and source-specific charges. Parallel work requires distinct actual compatible workers. Real care, sleep, recreation and treatment may overlap only where the exact procedures allow them; active labor cannot be credited twice.

If no provider can furnish the published preservation price, that offer is unavailable. Do not conjure preservatives or bill a second self-treatment charge for the same operation. Material freshness, access, volume, containment and original clocks still matter. Resurrection requires an actual available operative effect and finite contract; a service-price row cannot author a missing resurrection procedure.

Ordinary license administration takes one active hour with the actual desk, applicant and evidence. Before work the offer must state its posted fee, qualification, jurisdiction, scope and expiry; the source supplied no universal fee. Routine satisfied requirements issue without a roll. Uncertain evidence/persuasion uses its own procedure, and success grants nothing beyond the official's authority.

Patronage and information use the actual social-scene time/stakes and venue quote. A Core rumor-network fee grants its printed advantage only to real relevant Gather Information work using the appropriate existing skill; it creates no new proficiency. A private meeting protects only against the ordinary surveillance measures actually provided. Faction's 24-hour reporting checkpoints do not add labor or free service hours.

### FL-25 — Coins, unlawful operations, labor and rescue

Coin-service accounting and the fine use the confirmed correction reproduced in the player module. No service minimum is charged per coin, material variant or every 30-minute stage of a single commissioned bill. A private owned toolkit does not charge its owner a fictional desk fee. Cutting/reminting uses actual provider work and links spent coin identities to the real metal/output quantities without duplication.

Route undefined legacy skill names through existing methods: Streetwise uses CHA/Reputation or Persuasion for actual networks; Banking uses INT/Investigation or appropriate real commercial tools/records; Forgery uses the actual forgery kit with DEX or INT as the physical task warrants. No new universal skill or legal authority is created. Contact/terms/collection use declared social-scene time, movement uses routes, and document production its actual reviewed work. Do not insist on five rolls where one real transaction resolves the uncertainties, or roll an impossible stage.

Maintain stable Heat event IDs through fronts, repacking, splits and joins. Ended activity retains unresolved evidence/legal consequences. Never presume intent from ancestry, suspicion or a failed assay. Actual arrest, hearing, seizure and standing effects need their real scope.

Preserve person-market quotations only as reports of abusive/particular fictional institutions. They are not universal ancestry prices or a purchasable player-agency mechanic. The older captive 1/2–4 Load shorthand is superseded by the reviewed body/casualty rules, preserving the original as evidence.

If the source six-step rebellion clock is used for an actual coercive situation, add one step per distinct learned abuse, opportunity, allied-help or security-failure event. At 6, schedule the predeclared real escape/resistance action; do not manufacture mind control or a remote revolt. Evidence, reports, lawful authority and successful rescue remain separate facts. Literal soul-linked currency, religious, planar and deep-setting references are retained without interpretation.

### FL-26 — Advancement and the next descent

Award records preserve the frozen per-recipient gap, exact percentages, rounding, scene cap and unique actual contributions. Do not divide one party award into hidden unequal per-roll bonuses. A reward never promotes a pending spell, feat or class option. Use the supplied actual character schedules and thresholds, not a guessed level-cap extension.

The complete EX58 worked sequence is a synthetic source-bound example; it does not authenticate its people, route, buyer, coins, stock or factions as actual campaign facts. The full candidate must retain that 21-event sequence, 28 additional boundaries and 24 adjudicated proposal scenarios alongside its own complete records. This module's examples do not replace them.

Opening/Horn timing, retirement and seasonal references remain literal receipts. This chapter grants no new Cycle renewal, mind continuity, legacy infrastructure or cosmological interpretation. Keep public knowledge distinct from Director-sealed records. The separate full-candidate, runtime, human balance and whole-game flow reviews remain open.

### Worked chapter cases

#### EX03-01

**Situation:** A Tier-I fast return uncovers a real lien after intake.

**Expected result:** Move the same return to full intake, retaining the completed ten minutes and actual paid fees. Do not restart intake or charge both routes.

#### EX03-02

**Situation:** One representative is required for two different 30-minute desk proceedings.

**Expected result:** Without compatible actual participation or another qualified representative, schedule 60 minutes sequentially. Two staffed desks alone do not duplicate the person.

#### EX03-03

**Situation:** A 100-gp undamaged mundane item is sold under the actual Liked-buyer equipment schedule.

**Expected result:** Its offered reference is 60 gp before actual separate obligations. Do not also multiply by the Loop demand/provenance table.

#### EX03-04

**Situation:** An ordinary lot has a genuine 100-gp base, documented provenance, sought demand, sound condition and a real patient auction.

**Expected result:** The scoped commodity estimate is 137.5 gp. The buyer, auction wait and lawful sale remain actual prerequisites; this is not a guaranteed receipt.

#### EX03-05

**Situation:** A material’s actual source reference already includes its Degraded half-value adjustment.

**Expected result:** Do not apply the same loss again as the Loop damaged factor. Keep the existing condition adjustment and use 1 for its duplicate factor.

#### EX03-06

**Situation:** A named object is offered without an actual artifact BaseFunctionValue or GateTierMultiplier, and no finite buyer quote supplies them.

**Expected result:** It remains unvalued. A grand title or recipe Masterwork quality does not supply the missing factors.

#### EX03-07

**Situation:** The final exact purchase amount is 7/3 cp; a separate final sale receipt is also 7/3 cp.

**Expected result:** Pay 3 cp for the purchase and receive 2 cp for the sale. Do not round their internal multiplications independently.

#### EX03-08

**Situation:** Actual cargo receipts are120gp, direct tax6gp, mandatory inspection/quarantine2gp, and the accepted default guild share is20%.

**Expected result:** SponsorBase is112gp; share22.40gp; NetExpedition89.60gp. If the2gp was already paid, it is still counted only once.

#### EX03-09

**Situation:** The same episode has20.20gp of other actual operating costs and requires20gp retained from profit for its next reserve.

**Expected result:** Operating profit is69.40gp; profit available for distribution after that reserve is49.40gp. Neither is the gross120gp or NetExpedition89.60gp.

#### EX03-10

**Situation:** A single disposition is both licensed crafted goods and strategic regulated cargo.

**Expected result:** Use the one highest applicable8% bracket, not5%+8% or an additional routine3% levy.

#### EX03-11

**Situation:** A documented standard cargo policy insures100gp; first cumulative eligible loss is40gp, then reaches100gp.

**Expected result:** The once-per-episode20gp deductible gives first entitlement16gp, then total entitlement70gp. With16gp actually paid, the later payment is54gp, not70gp again.

#### EX03-12

**Situation:** A separate Core ransom-bond offer omits its annual premium or payout cap.

**Expected result:** It is an incomplete quote. Complete the real finite offer before purchase; do not invent terms or treat the standard cargo policy as a free rider.

#### EX03-13

**Situation:** A scene opens at standing+2 and contains actual ordinary proposals+2 and−2.

**Expected result:** The signed sum is0; recompute from opening+2, leaving+2. Applying one saturated change then the opposite must not create a different result.

#### EX03-14

**Situation:** A version-proven Cult Escapee relationship started at erroneous+1 and later earned+1.

**Expected result:** Replace only the initialization with−1 and replay earned+1, giving0. Unknown history would remain raw and flagged instead of reset.

#### EX03-15

**Situation:** A preservation provider accepts one coherent21-lb bill under the low-tier service reference.

**Expected result:** Charge three begun10-lb units at5sp, totaling15sp, for that accepted service. The actual compatible method/materials and lot work remain required.

#### EX03-16

**Situation:** A language learner already knows the needed script and completes actual instruction at the published reference rate.

**Expected result:** The full module needs280 active hours, credited in15-minute phases. The reference instruction amount is35gp; a shared script alone does not grant fluency.

#### EX03-17

**Situation:** A960-hour recipe is assigned two eight-hour productive Watches each day.

**Expected result:** It requires60 such productive days before its other exact gates. One Haven Turn cannot finish it, and actual interruptions may extend elapsed time.

#### EX03-18

**Situation:** One commissioned assay contains150 light coins under the10-lb weight cap.

**Expected result:** It needs two30-minute work units. The combined bill’s actual minimum assay fee applies once, not once per coin or work unit.

#### EX03-19

**Situation:** A skilled worker accepts the ordinary dangerous-expedition wage schedule.

**Expected result:** The reference is6gp per wage day for at most two eight-hour work Watches and protected Rest, not24 compulsory labor hours.

#### EX03-20

**Situation:** An Evidence Clock is3 and a genuinely new accessible lead earns Critical Success.

**Expected result:** Record the distinct+2 contribution but cap the clock at4. It supports a real warrant/rescue process; it does not itself free anyone or summon officers.

#### EX03-21

**Situation:** A scene freezes an XP gap of1200 and has a legitimate Severe25% award.

**Expected result:** The scene reference is300XP, with no recalculation of that frozen gap after an intervening level.

#### EX03-22

**Situation:** A frozen gap of125 has eligible scene percentages totaling at least60%.

**Expected result:** The exact ceiling is75XP; nearest10 with halves upward would give80, so preserve the cap and award70XP.

#### EX03-23

**Situation:** A return is appraised above the charter’s gross target but has made no sale and lacks reserve cash.

**Expected result:** Record the unrealized gross comparison. It neither pays wages nor proves the realized profit/reserve target or permits an otherwise unfunded next descent.

#### EX03-24

**Situation:** A false coin is detected in a holder’s documented innocent possession.

**Expected result:** Identify legitimate portions, actual authorized seizure and real service charges separately. A failed check or possession alone does not establish knowing fraud.

<a id="director-chapter-4"></a>
## 4. Social scenes and faction work

### SO-01 — Ask for a concrete outcome

Give the actual decision-maker a bounded want, leverage and limit before selecting a DC. Separate true scene facts from each participant's current knowledge and beliefs. Neither a role title nor a faction score proves access, funds, legal powers or knowledge. Do not offer a successful information result whose source cannot contain the answer.

Expose the stakes needed for an informed choice without publishing private motives. If the current approach cannot work, identify the observable obstacle or state that no roll is available for that concession. A successful roll delivers the feasible concession declared, even if an undisclosed personal motive makes the NPC dislike agreeing.

Maintain one scene identity and linked approach history. A closed negotiation does not require waiting for its future delivery or every later consequence. Those obligations retain their own deadlines and records.

### SO-02 — Resolve the declared uncertainty

The manual's full success, costly success and refusal/reversal are narrative possibilities. The four task degrees do not create a fourth secret price schedule. At a stated DC of 13, a final Success grants the promised concession; do not reserve it for a total of 23.

Record both an initial task failure and any separately accepted costly bargain. Require the actual payer, funds, authority and delivery. A failed task cannot automatically deduct the price of a declined alternative. An extraordinary die cannot bind an absent owner or establish a player's belief.

Use opposed checks for actual opposing attempts, not as a second defensive roll after a fixed-DC result. A feature's passive test or save is not replaced because another method would give a preferred outcome. Specific source exceptions remain attached to their exact feature.

### SO-03 — Choose a skill for the approach

Choose from the current twenty-skill foundation. Do not introduce a new universal Streetwise, Banking, Lore or faction-proficiency skill through this chapter. Existing narrowly reviewed aliases and knowledge methods keep their exact scope.

Keep fact, communicated claim, translation and inference distinct. A true statement may be unpersuasive for a real reason; a believed false report is still false. Failure need not create false certainty. Any critical extra information must be supported and declared.

Thief Feint's source-specific CHA or DEX+PB versus passive Insight is a combat procedure, not a general negotiation. Do not give it a second PB or substitute active opposed Insight. An actual spell or knowledge feature supplies only its own bounded information, including an unknown result when appropriate.

### SO-04 — Use standing as a relationship

The older Player seven-band table (Revered +5 through Hated −5) and Loop eleven-band access table remain complete source receipts. This is an explicit new choice to give neither table an automatic current d20 modifier, not a silent numeric conversion.

An evidenced companion-score-v1 +2 means current +2/Honored. The old Loop +2 meant Favored; an explicitly recorded RP-A01 mapping derives +1/Liked without overwriting the raw source or earned privileges. Unknown versions remain unresolved. A +3/Honored display does not reduce the score to +2.

The reputation module will bind all original faction/background records and save probes. This general player chapter exposes current policy and known relationships, not private faction descriptions, unknown save keys or hidden memberships. Do not use a category name as proof of an actual alliance or a particular institution's authority.

### SO-05 — Keep alignment-dependent questions separate

Preserve the entire original Player alignment passage, its identical/compatible/opposing categories and advantage/normal/disadvantage wording, including its incompatible and Harmony/Discord terminology. The full exact paragraphs remain in this module's internal source envelope. Do not resolve their meaning through an ordinary scene example.

The deferred questions are finite: the identity and meaning of the named axes; compatibility/opposition; the controlling source/version for any eventual modifier; whether ordinary policy should change; and literal deep-language, currency or faction links. Carry these to the agreed review after the verified GitHub push.

This is a temporary explicit no-automatic-modifier rule, not proof that all characters share an alignment or that all class predicates are now resolved. Apply a source-specific unresolved predicate only to its actual use, and do not invent a compatibility chart to unblock it.

### SO-06 — Keep one clock and a working channel

Record the source, recipient, channel, language/mode, perception, delivery interval and receipt time for information that matters to a deadline. Concurrent listening can fit one interval; serial relaying must have time. Do not backdate interpretation to the original speaker's start.

Silence the Caster has its own narrow reaction/component window; it is not a universal Silenced condition. Voluntary-silence oath histories follow LAX's distinct windows. A blocked deliberate speaking attempt can still count as a vocal act for that history while failing an audible-component requirement.

Source-specific bounded meaning bridges convey only what the exact effect grants. Tongues III does not gain an invented hearing prerequisite absent from its card, while effects that do require hearing still require it. Speech, received meaning, known language, component performance and truthful knowledge are separate facts.

### SO-07 — Help with the work; retry when something changes

Before granting Help, identify what the helper does and reserve their actual interval. In an encounter keep the initiative marker rather than converting it to a whole social scene. A physically separate or uninformed ally cannot contribute merely because they share a roster.

Record why a new approach is different. A letter can matter because it contains new authentic authority or evidence, not because it has a new document ID. Another speaker alone does not erase an earlier approach. Resolve a new feasible proposal on its own terms without secretly refunding earlier costs.

Shattered Mind Boon IV's failed INT/WIS reroll remains its actual acquired feature with its own restrictions. It does not authorize a CHA retry, another scene cap, an unspent resource refund or a replacement of an already closed event.

### SO-08 — Apply the exact magical effect

Retain the complete actual sources alongside these interface limits. These are reminders for applying acquired features, not replacement cards or new selections:

| Interface | Keep its exact boundary |
|---|---|
| Oracle Tongues I | Receptive spoken-mortal comprehension and the bonus for actually hearing eligible allies; no universal fluency/literacy |
| Tongues III / IV / V | III's yes/no/unknown and player refusal; IV's genuine meaningful-exchange time and targets; V's command, hearing, save, restrictions and success-damage branch |
| Tongues overlapping voices | Its exact social −2 and Action suppression for ten minutes when the source conditions apply |
| Heaven's Decree / Sovereign's Ascendance | The target retains obey-in-the-safest-way **or** backlash; no forced confession, belief or allegiance |
| Aura of Authority | Obstructing a lie neither forces truthful speech nor supplies translation |
| False Life | Its Insight intervention is the particular healing gate, not general social suspicion or a universal restriction on care |
| Paladin Wave | The later fulfilled long-rest amendment remains controlling |
| Mage/Wizard Arcane Insight, Cleric Divine Insight, Oracle knowledge/omen/scout features | Their bounded supported subject, exact costs and possible unknown result |
| Ranger Pathfinder's Mark / Swift Traverse | A bearing is not a clear route or teleport; paid tactical Dash does not double a travel day |
| Ranger quiet footsteps | No automatic silence for companions, gear or speech, and no blanket removal of tracking |
| Soldier Quick Deploy | Its limited cover does not become transferable camp lumber or a palisade |
| Druid forms | Own body history and actual limited movement/attacks; no permanent resources manufactured by transforming |
| Thief Feint | Its literal passive-Insight combat procedure, not a negotiation |
| Warrior Intimidation / morale hooks | Actual source windows; a morale penalty is not automatically Pressure, a save modifier or an Escape/Pursuit penalty |
| Tinker, AEX and haulers | Distinct commands, one-command budgets, servicing and repairs; TEX Efficient Recycling retains its no-enemy-cause rule; the 43 crafted devices gain no invented deployment RP cost |

The internal envelope preserves all seventeen complete class datasets, 1,223 cards and the 94 selected comparison records with their existing statuses. Comparing a card does not activate it. The ordinary authored index remains 412 reviewed and 436 pending; the 136 standalone procedures retain their complete operational records rather than a lossy legacy conversion. Pending spells and feats are not enabled by this chapter.

A secret meaning, unavailable active spell or missing world record restricts only the particular use needing it. It is not permission to invent the missing feature or reopen every source decision.

### SO-09 — Record agreements and respect table choices

Keep practical table agreements in the shared Session Zero record. Do not put personal reasons for boundaries or accommodations in a fictional ledger. The ordinary scene record stores only the relevant permission and its scope.

An NPC may face fictional pressure while still having the stated limits and actual powers of that NPC. Winning a feasible concession does not create a legal power to perform a different one. Keep an offered agreement, accepted agreement and completed performance as separate states.

An effect's willing-target predicate must be satisfied as that source requires at the required time. A social success, admission to a meeting or ride in a cart cannot be reused as blanket consent for unrelated procedures.

### SO-10 — Let consequences reach the people who react

Link each receiving institution's reaction to the originating act and its actual receipt event. Repeated messengers can strengthen knowledge without manufacturing additional underlying deeds. Separate an individual's relationship from their institution's standing.

For a scene opening at +2, a proposed +2 yields +3 after the scale cap: applied +1, overflow +1. A distinct −2 proposal in that same scene makes the algebraic proposed sum zero, so recomputation from opening +2 returns +2. The earlier overflow cannot be spent in another scene.

Do not compensate a failed persuasion roll with invented reputation. A independently meaningful witnessed good deed can still matter, but needs its own established significance and attribution. Unknown institutional goals or a shared registry label do not justify a universal allied/enemy reaction. Preserve raw source versions and earned history under the reputation contract.

### SO-11 — Faction activity takes real work

Before resolving a checkpoint, nominate at most three distinct watched factions. Do not swap in a fourth mid-checkpoint for another slot. The next watch list can change, but no new name creates retroactive staff or work. Keep the canonical day origin from the expedition clock; do not restart a 24-hour timer on each arrival.

For each action record: persistent action/objective IDs; named responsible actors; starting knowledge and its source; location, route and access; actual tools, materials, money and permissions; committed staff intervals; positive duration; the governing resolution rule; success/failure consequences; and completion prerequisites. An in-progress action continues under its existing ID. A checkpoint acknowledges actual work, not a transferable Action token.

Routine feasible, uncontested work needs no invented roll. Contested or dangerous work uses the actual social, task, route or encounter procedure. A raid does not resolve an absent battle with one generic die. Research cannot ignore its actual formula, language, subject or evidence requirements. Concealment can hide finite evidence or access without deleting true history.

One actor cannot work two incompatible fronts or treat someone while claiming the same time as rest. Independent staff can work in parallel only if really assigned and supplied. A faction's advertised backer is not unlimited funds or secret intelligence.

Keep completion time separate from report time and mark each result as reported once. Publish only information the actual audience can receive. A private project can have a visible consequence without revealing its whole plan or dossier. These procedures are authored Director tools; they do not claim that companion software simulates offscreen behavior.

### SO-12 — Rival objectives leave a history

Keep every field in the original rival sheet:

| Source field | Record |
|---|---|
| Public identity | Name and identity available to the stated audience |
| Backer | Actual patron/source of support, with its knowledge boundary |
| Current depth | Actual position in the relevant route |
| Primary objective | The specific goal being pursued |
| Preferred method | The means the group tends to use |
| Professed limit | The line they claim they will not cross |
| Need from the party | A concrete potential exchange or dependency |
| Feared disclosure | What they fear the party will learn; keep private until actually learned |
| Current relationship | The source-qualified relationship and relevant history |

Add, rather than replace these fields with, actor IDs, real assets, location/route, known information and its source, resources/obligations, ongoing action IDs and audience permissions. Give the rival a practical strength and weakness. Do not use it as an omniscient counter to player plans. A generic standing category among the 44 source records is not a fully staffed expedition. New campaign institution IDs require the reputation binding, not a history invented from their names.

For every clock retain its objective ID, chosen length, trigger, starting time, all qualifying event IDs and receipt times, current segments, finite consequence, prerequisites and actual resolution. A Depth threshold cannot bypass an impassable route. A Claim threshold cannot replace authentic proof or an authorized hearing. Grudge schedules its real hostile action; it grants no immediate extra attack. Collapse can cause the declared real withdrawal, fracture or desperate bargain without deleting actors or property.

When a threshold is blocked, state the actual missing prerequisite and retain the clock's history. A new genuine event can supply it; repeated reports cannot. A real reversal can change the current objective through a new event, but negative progress does not erase an earlier deed or refund resources.

All 24 original social proposal scenarios and GPT's individual final dispositions are preserved in this module. They are source-bound examples, not evidence of actual campaign events or software execution. Keep private backer and feared-disclosure fields out of the public projection.

### Worked chapter cases

#### EX04-01

**Situation:** An authorized clerk willingly offers a routine viewing appointment.

**Expected result:** No roll is needed. Record the appointment and actual viewing time; no extra benefit appears from asking to roll.

#### EX04-02

**Situation:** A guard has the warehouse key but no authority to sell its stock.

**Expected result:** Explain the missing authority before a sale check. Access to a key does not make the sale feasible.

#### EX04-03

**Situation:** A promised DC13 concession is met with final total13 and no natural degree change.

**Expected result:** Grant the announced success; no hidden DC23, additional fee or second defensive check.

#### EX04-04

**Situation:** A fixed task has DC13, total3 and natural1.

**Expected result:** It is Critical Failure at the margin and remains at that bounded degree after the shift. Apply only its declared consequence.

#### EX04-05

**Situation:** A possible fixed task has DC35, natural20 and final total20.

**Expected result:** The margin starts at Critical Failure; natural20 improves it to Failure. It does not compel obedience.

#### EX04-06

**Situation:** A deception and actual scrutiny both total17; the initiating die is natural20.

**Expected result:** The opposed tie preserves the prior state. No task degree shift changes the total or adds an opposed critical outcome.

#### EX04-07

**Situation:** An actual CHA/Reputation appeal has CHA+2, applicable PB+2 and known standing+2.

**Expected result:** The eligible ordinary modifier is+4, not+6. Recognized standing is evidence for the approach, not another numeric bonus.

#### EX04-08

**Situation:** A successful Insight test observes hesitation but the private letter is inaccessible.

**Expected result:** Give the predeclared supported inference. Do not reveal the letter or claim certainty that the available evidence cannot support.

#### EX04-09

**Situation:** A Performance succeeds at entertaining an actual audience.

**Expected result:** Deliver the audience response. A separate property transfer or permit still needs actual authority and its own feasible approach.

#### EX04-10

**Situation:** One old save says companion-score-v1+2; another says Loop eleven-band+2.

**Expected result:** Preserve both raw scores and versions. The first is+2/Honored; an explicitly accepted Loop mapping derives+1/Liked, retaining its historical Favored meaning and earned privileges.

#### EX04-11

**Situation:** A saved faction key is absent while another is explicitly numeric zero.

**Expected result:** Keep those distinct. Only the real zero is a recorded neutral score; do not fabricate an absent entry.

#### EX04-12

**Situation:** A current+3/Honored actor displays only the label Honored.

**Expected result:** The underlying current score stays+3. It is not normalized to+2 or added to the social die.

#### EX04-13

**Situation:** Two negotiators publicly share a civic goal.

**Expected result:** That communicated goal can be leverage. It establishes no cosmic alignment identity and adds no automatic advantage.

#### EX04-14

**Situation:** An older paragraph lists alignment compatibility bonuses with unresolved meanings.

**Expected result:** Retain the complete paragraph as a receipt; use the explicit no-automatic-alignment-modifier rule in this ordinary chapter.

#### EX04-15

**Situation:** One acquired oath use depends on an undefined deep predicate.

**Expected result:** Record that particular dependency without inventing its meaning or blocking unrelated ordinary trade and conversation.

#### EX04-16

**Situation:** Four participants conduct an exchange whose complete interpretation fits ten minutes.

**Expected result:** Advance the shared clock600seconds, not2400. Each actual participant interval is still occupied.

#### EX04-17

**Situation:** A3-second warning is followed by a3-second rendition.

**Expected result:** The final listener receives it at6seconds. It cannot affect a decision already resolved at4seconds.

#### EX04-18

**Situation:** A creature uses4seconds of free speech, then has an extra turn in the same numbered round.

**Expected result:** Only2seconds of the six-second free allowance remain. Further six-second segments need actual available Actions/permissions.

#### EX04-19

**Situation:** The helper’s next regular start passes before the relevant check.

**Expected result:** The initiative Help expires. A later social check cannot retain it merely because the conversation continues.

#### EX04-20

**Situation:** One helper is requested for two simultaneous ten-minute negotiations in separate rooms.

**Expected result:** They cannot qualify for both full intervals. Schedule separate work or use another actual helper.

#### EX04-21

**Situation:** Five party members repeat the same refused appeal; afterward an authentic new authority letter arrives.

**Expected result:** The repeated unchanged appeals create no retries. The new letter may justify a distinct feasible approach with new declared time/stakes.

#### EX04-22

**Situation:** A Charmed clerk still lacks authority to surrender the employer’s archive.

**Expected result:** Charm can supply its actual social advantage only for a possible check. It does not create authority or obedience.

#### EX04-23

**Situation:** A Frightened creature loses sight of its still-active fear source.

**Expected result:** Its visibility-dependent disadvantage ceases. It still cannot willingly move closer while Frightened.

#### EX04-24

**Situation:** A target resists an actual magical control payload.

**Expected result:** A social roll cannot recreate that payload. An independently feasible ordinary request retains its ordinary limits.

#### EX04-25

**Situation:** A failed check offers a predeclared feasible1gp alternative; the player declines.

**Expected result:** Keep the failed result and refusal. Do not deduct the100cp or invent a debt.

#### EX04-26

**Situation:** A player agreed to a character’s carriage but not to surgery.

**Expected result:** The ride does not satisfy surgery’s actual permission. Establish the separate predicate when needed.

#### EX04-27

**Situation:** A player invokes the agreed pause signal during a coercion scene.

**Expected result:** Pause/revise under Session Zero with no fictional punishment or demand for a personal explanation.

#### EX04-28

**Situation:** An institution/scene opens at+2 and receives a distinct ordinary+2 proposal.

**Expected result:** End at+3 with applied+1 and overflow+1. Replaying the same underlying event changes nothing; overflow cannot be banked.

#### EX04-29

**Situation:** The same scene later also has an independent qualifying−2 proposal.

**Expected result:** Recompute signed+2−2 from opening+2. The resulting score is+2, not+1 from sequential saturation.

#### EX04-30

**Situation:** A private rescue reaches a relevant institution by messenger at noon.

**Expected result:** The institution cannot act on it at11:00. Record actual receipt, attribution and that institution’s own response; other groups do not automatically learn it.

#### EX04-31

**Situation:** A genuine return occurs at the campaign’s completed24-hour boundary.

**Expected result:** Coalesce the two reasons into one checkpoint, with at most three preselected watched factions and one eligible action each.

#### EX04-32

**Situation:** An eight-hour recruitment project has completed two hours when the party briefly recrosses Haven’s boundary.

**Expected result:** Six active hours remain. The crossing grants neither a new episode checkpoint nor a completed substitute recruit.

#### EX04-33

**Situation:** A funded project actually finishes at08:00; its messenger arrives09:00; the checkpoint is24:00.

**Expected result:** The consequence exists at08:00, recipients learn it at09:00, and the checkpoint acknowledges the same completion once without recreating it.

#### EX04-34

**Situation:** A Claim clock at2/4 receives one genuinely new qualifying document and three reports of it.

**Expected result:** Advance to3/4 once. Preserve the reports as information history, not three more segments.

#### EX04-35

**Situation:** A Depth clock reaches its declared threshold while the required route remains impassable.

**Expected result:** Retain threshold reached and the actual missing passage prerequisite. Do not teleport the rival or roll an invented completion check.

#### EX04-36

**Situation:** A Grudge clock reaches4/4 with a declared future ambush response.

**Expected result:** Schedule that real action with actors, resources and travel. It does not grant an immediate attack or resolve the encounter.
