<a id="director-chapter-5"></a>
## 5. Mounts, vehicles and transport

### TR-01 — Choose an actual mount

Keep the individual's original body history through purchase, hire, boarding, rescue, bond and sale. A healthy profile does not prove that a merchant's specimen is healthy, willing, trained or available. Significance cannot be changed after a fatal outcome.

The new threat reference level is solely for the expedition encounter-budget comparison. It is neither CR nor class level. An effect requiring a source CR still needs that actual missing predicate. Do not substitute threat reference, purchase value or carrying rating for a bond prerequisite or harvest table.

The catalogue's Load and draw limits are separately authored. They do not follow an inferred Strength formula and cannot be increased by recalculating one. Apply later actual source exceptions only where they govern this same individual.

### TR-02 — Control and training

Record the controlling relation, the trained function and cue, seated rider, available hand/channel, animal willingness, actor-cap usage and actual turn. Ordinary riding cues are rechecked at execution; this explicitly differs from a protected already-issued attached companion command that may survive owner incapacity.

Do not import TI04 device binding, a shared companion Bonus Action or an attack allowance into ordinary riding. Carriage and ship control have the separately paid Actions below. Unattended lead/follow is not a programmable tactical routine or unlimited work.

Training is an actual bounded course. Its maximum four hours/day applies to that animal, not a new cap per instructor or per course name. Forty hours means at least ten such training days. Safe completion grants no immunity to later fear, fatigue, pain, anatomy restrictions or changed conditions.

### TR-03 — One complete mount turn

Record actor ID, numbered round, regular-turn ID, original slot, prospective next-round slot, control history, and movement/Action/Reaction spent. Keep condition and death-save ordering attached to the actual creature.

Ordinary ridden turns are whole turns. The coordinated puller reservation for a drawn vehicle below is a specific exception for one linked vehicle movement, not permission to split every mount's turn. Travel and initiative transitions preserve real movement/work and do not duplicate ticks or rewards.

### TR-04 — Mounting, dismounting and losing the seat

Predeclare the actual saddle point, approach and preferred dismount ordering where relevant. Record the triggering source event so one forced-and-Prone result does not create two saves. Do not infer another trigger from a renamed template or the rider's own Prone condition.

Success is not immunity to falling when no supporting body remains. Keep actual seat compatibility and the control predicates separate from the save. Resolve height, falling, collision and support through their own real sources instead of inserting an automatic unseat-damage packet.

### TR-05 — Position, areas and opportunity attacks

Keep the mount footprint, rider seat, actual movement path and each target's defenses. At a provoking edge, validate the chosen target before spending/rolling the reaction. Actual legal defensive reactions belong to the chosen target; no mount-and-rider pooled defense is created.

The same movement cannot provoke twice merely because both rider and mount moved. Turning travel into initiative is not a movement event. A positional aura reads actual body locations even when carriage does not qualify for a self-powered movement benefit.

### TR-06 — Movement belongs to the actor who performs it

Preserve the literal Dragoon protected interface and complete engine alongside this new interpretation; the old sentence did not explicitly say self-propelled. The fifteen-foot Momentum trigger and twenty-foot casting interaction are separate gates. A legal dismount, self-powered move/leap and personal attack can satisfy the actual engine, without counting the same path twice.

Keep both complete pending feat records, their original flags and current full-review-pending status. Old needsReview:false is not proof of current review. No broad text match across 1,223 class cards establishes movement eligibility. Exact position, actor, turn, movement budget and activation predicates are required.

Nothing here replaces closed companion commands, recipe devices, servicing, recycling, recharge or source-specific casting procedures.

### TR-07 — Tack, food, water and boarding

All fourteen original tack/drawn-vehicle/service rows retain their full source records, including comma placeholders, null numerical weights and source status. New physical quantities are separate fields. A service is not a half-pound object; unknown weight is not zero.

Record actual fitting, workers, hands, anatomy, item HP, stock, nutrition and custody. A caretaker cannot supervise incompatible remote pens at the same time. The ten-minute calm care allowance is not emergency veterinary treatment.

Barding cost, mass, HP and worn AC use distinct rules. Apply the actual sized armor reference once for repair, not the animal's purchase value or HP. Fitted tack can be repaired from its actual primary body and compatible subordinate parts; neither repair nor a saddle creates a missing animal.

### TR-08 — Drawn vehicles and the two sleds

Record the coordinated movement reservation as one exceptional vehicle event, not ordinary interleaved riding turns. Each puller needs a legal existing movement budget and actual control; the vehicle does not create free offensive caravan actors. One Action or movement contribution cannot pay a different task too.

Cart, Wagon, Carriage, Chariot and Player Sled retain their complete catalogue dimensions, seats, primary structures and terrain. Do not equate a frame's HP with payload or use an invented five-times-Strength towing formula. An indivisible blocked bend remains a real route problem.

A disabled frame strands its actual cargo until a feasible unload, transfer, support or tow occurs. HP 0 alone does not destroy occupants, contents or source history. Collision, dislodgement and falling are separate actual events with their declared consequences.

### TR-09 — Watercraft need working crews

The full profiles preserve each exact active-mode Speed, mph, minimum depth, geometry, occupant count and original-body repair requirements. New operating values do not fill in or overwrite the old source blanks. “Warship” grants no ballista or separate combat-stat package.

Track all paid crew contributions, current availability, the single numbered-round drive event and the actual resulting path. Passive passenger displacement is not personal movement expenditure, Dragoon Momentum or the mounted opportunity-attack exception. Passengers may do compatible tasks only when the actual environment permits; a moving ship is not automatically a full workshop or safe rest site.

No vessel quote supplies a conveniently present rescue boat or crew. Plan escape from actual positions, remaining time, usable lines, boats, bodies and Actions. Real support can preserve a disabled wreck for repair; its contents and history remain real even if submerged.

### TR-10 — Porters, bearers and hired pack animals

Keep service scope separate from anatomy and ownership. A contracted six Load may fit a mule's body but not its actual current burden or route. The rate purchases an agreed service interval, not feed or unlimited extra work unless the offer really includes it.

Apply the service-specific price before the one accepted danger multiplier. Do not add the generic 1/3/10 gp wage schedule to the named porter/bearer rate or double once per danger adjective. Maintain actual paid/owed status without automatic borrowing.

Calm loading uses the transfer schedule below. Dangerous travel uses the real route/encounter procedure, not another service roll that charges the same consequence twice. Actual workers' consent, refusal and information remain distinct from inert inventory.

### TR-11 — The bound hauler and specialist services

The hauler is bound to the separate core.transport.hauler profile. The 500 gp quote and 20/30 Load references are historical; its mass, body, oil, maintenance, control and repair are explicitly new additions. Do not replace these with a class machine's rules because both are called a hauler.

Track exact accumulated moving time, fuel fractions, refill/consumption events, service-due state and actual completed work. A partial service is not completed service, and one work interval cannot also restore HP or recharge another device. A damaged required mechanism must be made serviceable through its real repair before routine service can qualify.

Caravan availability, a real slot and the actual specialist teleport are world inputs. Do not invent infinite capacity, a current spell, post-Opening access, planar permission or spatial-container capacity from a price. Preserve Living/Changed, Pact and deep-setting labels literally for the later review.

### TR-12 — People, stretchers and cargo transfers

Keep distinct body, gear, containers and shares. Petrification changes only the body pounds and never turns six casualty Load into sixty. Reusing a transformed character's personal meld exemption on the outside carrier would omit real weight.

The stretcher's patient admission and each carrier's total burden are independent checks. Its frame and every gear item still count. An unstable transfer can fail even when numerical limits fit; conversely no roll is needed to invent uncertainty in a genuinely safe supported lift.

Preserve voluntary, unconscious, restrained and disputed-custody states separately. Unknown actual landing space or insufficient support is not repaired by a cargo tag. A dead body remains the same history-bearing body under its real lifecycle, not a fresh generic loot bundle.

### TR-13 — One route rate, with real transfers

Keep route identity and fraction complete before changing the limiting rate. Record actual physical quality independently of Unknown/Sketched/Mapped/Secured mapping knowledge and Risk 1–5. Apply the 480-minute reference only once to the actual work tranche.

Do not use a zero required-carrier speed as a division shortcut or silently omit that actor. Determine the real transfer, abandonment or repair first. Source-specific route/hazard rules override only their actual scope.

For declared cargo loss, retain the original at-risk manifest, event, chosen eligible lots/portions and indivisibility remainder. Do not stack the Core illustration with a Loop consequence unless they are genuinely independent declared effects. Living/Changed and Pact Interdiction remain literal specialist/deep-setting receipts, without invented geography, causes or permissions.

### TR-14 — Recover living creatures; repair actual structures

Distinguish living treatment, mundane structural repair, recipe-magical restoration, the new hauler bridge and one-time emergency shoring. Each uses its real eligibility and source. A payment is not evidence that a missing body, material, worker or worksite exists.

For a damaged vessel, record damage-event ID, HP-0 time, original deadline, shoring eligibility, workers, uninterrupted interval, supplies, result, any sixty-minute hold deadline, actual support and evacuation. The hull does not regain movement from successful shoring. A failed or interrupted attempt cannot backdate completion beyond the old deadline.

Track rescued actors and goods individually, with actual custody, remaining conditions and source history. Keep family admission, overload, control-loss, interrupted-movement, damage/HP-0, repair/rescue and provenance examples as authored expectations. They do not claim that live transport software or human balance has been tested.

### Worked chapter cases

#### TRX-01

**Situation:** A willing animal completes the40-hour ordinary training course at its maximum4 credited hours/day.

**Expected result:** At least10 training days are required; two instructors cannot create an8-hour animal training day.

#### TRX-02

**Situation:** A rider acts, then its mount begins its whole turn and moves into a new reach.

**Expected result:** The rider has no inserted free turn segment. A previously legal readied Action still needs its Reaction and actual trigger.

#### TRX-03

**Situation:** A rider’s current walkingSpeed is31ft with15ft of regular movement left.

**Expected result:** One ordinary mount costs15ft and can fit the budget if all other predicates hold. Speed1 costs1ft; Speed0 cannot mount.

#### TRX-04

**Situation:** One source event pushes the mount and knocks it Prone.

**Expected result:** One binaryDC10DEX seat save, not two. Rider success preserves seating only where the real body still supports it.

#### TRX-05

**Situation:** A mount voluntarily leaves an enemy’s reach; the seat is outside that enemy’s legal reach.

**Expected result:** The enemy may attack the mount if its own predicates hold, but cannot choose an unreachable rider. OneReaction buys one attack.

#### TRX-06

**Situation:** Only the rider used Disengage before the mount’s voluntary provoking movement.

**Expected result:** The mount’s provocation remains. Only the mount’s own valid Disengage removes the mounted target-choice opportunity for its movement.

#### TRX-07

**Situation:** A Dragoon rides30ft and then casts without self-propelled movement on its own turn.

**Expected result:** No Momentum movement gain and no new transport-bridge twenty-foot casting benefit; physical position is still real for range/auras.

#### TRX-08

**Situation:** A Dragoon actually travels20ft under its own power on its current regular turn before a legal Weave cast.

**Expected result:** Apply the actual one movement gain if its engine predicates hold and the separate Dissonance−1 minimum0. NoRP discount or extra threshold gain.

#### TRX-09

**Situation:** A reviewed armor baseline costs50gp and weighs20lb; its compatible Large barding is selected.

**Expected result:** The ordinary references are200gp and80lb. Do not charge400gp by also applying general Large pricing.

#### TRX-10

**Situation:** A mule with600lb draw rating pulls a200lb cart plus100lb total payload.

**Expected result:** Gross300lb equals half draw rating, so the strictly-above-half draw condition alone does not apply. Its own positive carried burden at half its separate carry cap still does.

#### TRX-11

**Situation:** A sail mode’s stated rate is4mph with adverse but usable wind and a1mph countercurrent.

**Expected result:** Relative propulsion is2mph and along-route ground progress1mph under these stated vectors; no second wind multiplier or unstated calm assumption.

#### TRX-12

**Situation:** The ship has no usable wind and the selected mode is sail only.

**Expected result:** Sail self-propulsion is0. The real current may still drift it; purchase, crew Actions or a high check cannot invent wind.

#### TRX-13

**Situation:** One porter accepts the named2sp/day service and ordinary double danger rate.

**Expected result:** The agreed rate is4sp for that service day, not generic dangerous unskilled wages plus4sp. Actual contracted cargo and body limits still apply.

#### TRX-14

**Situation:** A genuineTierII teleport service accepts a single commissioned2.1Load bill, with an actual operative effect and endpoints.

**Expected result:** Round the total to3Load once, for600gp under this reference. Without the actual effect/endpoint, the price alone supplies no teleport.

#### TRX-15

**Situation:** A180lb Medium body becomesPetrified while carrying20lb gear.

**Expected result:** An external carrier bears1820lb and the same6casualtyLoads plus equipmentLoad. Do not multiply gear or casualtyLoad by10.

#### TRX-16

**Situation:** Three separate fitting40lb bundles and one indivisible120lb statue require calm loading.

**Expected result:** The three bundles require three actual one-minute units; the statue needs its real team/hoist and separately declared positive duration, not automatic fifty-pound slicing.

#### TRX-17

**Situation:** A480-minute nominal route tranche hasContested quality1.25 and an actual2mph limiting convoy.

**Expected result:** It requires900 active minutes. No extra480 minutes or duplicate hourly distance is awarded.

#### TRX-18

**Situation:** Half aKnown route tranche is completed at3mph, then the limiting rate falls to1.5mph.

**Expected result:** The first240 active minutes remain completed; the other half needs480 more, for720 total. Do not retroactively double completed work.

#### TRX-19

**Situation:** StoredPressure is6 on aContested ordinary travel-complication check.

**Expected result:** EffectivePressure clamps to6 for that one check; storedPressure remains6 and no extra roll is created.

#### TRX-20

**Situation:** A300HP mundane hull has an eligible original body and full qualified repair inputs.

**Expected result:** Ordinary self-repair requires30 active work hours, not one eight-hourWatch. A genuine alternative complete service quote is not charged another25%.

### TRF-MOUNT

#### TRF-MOUNT-01 — admission

**Situation:** A Small rider, fitting saddle and gear fit a willing trained Medium Pony in both pounds and Load.

**Expected result:** The body/seat prerequisites can qualify. A Medium rider is still too large for that saddle rule even if equally light.

#### TRF-MOUNT-02 — overload

**Situation:** A mule with a420lb/42Load profile has421lb of actual supported rider, tack and cargo.

**Expected result:** Routine carriage is unavailable even if its Load is below42. Transfer a real item or choose another feasible method.

#### TRF-MOUNT-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** A seated rider becomes incapacitated before the controlled animal executes its cue.

**Expected result:** Recheck the cue. The animal retains its existing turn but defensively Dodges if able and uses only its own immediate-safety movement, without a new player attack order.

#### TRF-MOUNT-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** A mount has taken its round’s regular turn; a new rider mounts it afterward.

**Expected result:** No second turn or fresh budgets occur. A valid new linked position begins next numbered round.

#### TRF-MOUNT-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** An ordinary mount registered as significant before battle reaches0HP.

**Expected result:** Use its actual Core dying/death-save and Wound history. Do not retrospectively designate it insignificant or replace it with the different Ranger Beast lifecycle.

#### TRF-MOUNT-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** A living injured mount is brought to a paid stable; the owner offers its quarter-price as repair.

**Expected result:** Only actual creature treatment/rest/healing can recover it. Stabling or object repair cannot heal or resurrect the individual.

#### TRF-MOUNT-07 — provenance

**Situation:** The same rescued mule is later sold or enters a legitimate Ranger bond.

**Expected result:** Retain its original identity, body, injury, custody and remains history. The source-specific new lifecycle does not create a second animal.

### TRF-BARDING

#### TRF-BARDING-01 — admission

**Situation:** A Large trained mount is fitted with an actual reviewed ordinary armor model.

**Expected result:** Use baseline price×4, mass×4 and armorHP×2, with the model’s actual AC formula, Dexterity cap and restrictions. Do not multiply its price by the general Large factor again.

#### TRF-BARDING-02 — overload

**Situation:** The chosen barding makes the animal’s actual supported burden exceed its profile limit.

**Expected result:** Wearing competence cannot waive weight. Remove or transfer real burden before routine movement.

#### TRF-BARDING-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** The animal loses its valid riding relation while wearing barding.

**Expected result:** Armor supplies no controlling cue, willingness or attack. Use the actual loss-of-control procedure.

#### TRF-BARDING-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** A form change makes the currently fitted armor incompatible.

**Expected result:** Record actual fit and body effects; transformation does not resize or refit it. No movement is granted by changing the armor label.

#### TRF-BARDING-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** The barding’s actual armor body becomes Broken.

**Expected result:** Resolve that object under its armor rules separately from the animal’s HP. Its damage is not a duplicate creature Wound.

#### TRF-BARDING-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** Compatible original barding survives and a trained worker has real replacement fittings.

**Expected result:** Repair that sized armor using its own material/value/time requirements, not the whole animal’s price or maximum HP.

#### TRF-BARDING-07 — provenance

**Situation:** The old Barding row has a comma/blank and null numerical weight.

**Expected result:** Preserve that original record. The current selected model and new sizing values are separate operating data, not a recovered source weight.

### TRF-TACK

#### TRF-TACK-01 — admission

**Situation:** A pair of saddlebags has a fitting20lb/0.5ft³ load in each real cell.

**Expected result:** Admission also requires actual shape, balance, closure and an animal able to carry the4lb bags and contents.

#### TRF-TACK-02 — overload

**Situation:** One saddlebag cell contains21lb and the other19lb.

**Expected result:** The40lb combined total does not excuse the first cell’s20lb limit. Repack a real fitting portion if possible.

#### TRF-TACK-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** A rider loses their only usable rein hand and the individual has no trained tactile alternative.

**Expected result:** A bridle’s presence does not preserve a usable cue. Recheck control at the animal’s execution.

#### TRF-TACK-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** A saddle fitting is interrupted before its actual fitting work is complete.

**Expected result:** Retain elapsed work/cost and the actual attachment state. Do not declare a safe usable seat merely because the item is owned.

#### TRF-TACK-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** The actual saddle body is destroyed while its rider is supported by it.

**Expected result:** Resolve the real loss of support and landing. ItemHP0 is neither an automatic fixed injury packet nor magical continued seating.

#### TRF-TACK-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** An intact main bag body needs new straps; a qualified worker has actual compatible materials.

**Expected result:** Use the ordinary actual object repair procedure. Repair restores that item, not lost contents or another copy of its cargo.

#### TRF-TACK-07 — provenance

**Situation:** A pack saddle and a military saddle change owners.

**Expected result:** Keep their distinct item identities/functions. Neither the pack saddle’s name nor the military name grants a rider seat or feat beyond its actual profile.

### TRF-PROVISION

#### TRF-PROVISION-01 — admission

**Situation:** A real camel-day feed purchase at5cp supplies12lb of compatible stock, and a fitting24h stall is available.

**Expected result:** Record actual feed and stall separately. The camel still needs32pints of real water unless the quoted service actually includes it.

#### TRF-PROVISION-02 — overload

**Situation:** A pen fits a dog but cannot physically accommodate the named Elephant.

**Expected result:** Paying the posted5sp cannot admit the Elephant; find an actual fitting pen. A service has no invented cargo or half-pound capacity.

#### TRF-PROVISION-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** The caretaker is unavailable and no replacement has actually taken custody.

**Expected result:** Payment does not create supervision, a handler or an animal command. Keep the real custody gap and consequences.

#### TRF-PROVISION-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** A caravan stops during a day in which some feed and water have already been consumed.

**Expected result:** Keep consumed stock and elapsed work. A transport-mode change or second gate crossing grants no second free ration or new paid day.

#### TRF-PROVISION-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** Stored feed is spoiled and the stable building becomes inaccessible.

**Expected result:** Record actual provision loss and service availability; neither service row has fictional creatureHP or a universal repairable zero-HP body.

#### TRF-PROVISION-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** An injured animal reaches safe boarding and needs treatment beyond ordinary cleaning/supervision.

**Expected result:** Use actual additional care, workers, supplies and time. The included ten caretaker minutes do not cure Wounds or replace emergency treatment.

#### TRF-PROVISION-07 — provenance

**Situation:** A bundled boarding offer includes identifiable feed and water quantities.

**Expected result:** Charge/consume those actual included quantities once, retain the agreement and custody interval, and do not bill the same provided feed twice.

### TRF-DRAWN

#### TRF-DRAWN-01 — admission

**Situation:** An actual200lb Cart carries a300lb fitting payload under its30Load cap; a trained mule has a600lb draw rating.

**Expected result:** Gross draw is500lb, with the mule’s own burden checked separately. The route, harness, driver and seat/occupant limits must also qualify.

#### TRF-DRAWN-02 — overload

**Situation:** That Cart is assigned301lb of payload while the puller could tow the resulting gross weight.

**Expected result:** The Cart’s300lb payload cap still prevents routine admission. A stronger puller cannot expand the bed or frame rating.

#### TRF-DRAWN-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** The driver paid an Action, but a required harness breaks before the last puller contribution becomes available.

**Expected result:** The coordinated drive does not resolve without current feasibility. Retain the spent Action and actual body/cargo positions.

#### TRF-DRAWN-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** A driver swaps after one linked vehicle event has already moved this round.

**Expected result:** The new driver does not grant a second event or restore spent puller movement. Preserve actor and frame history.

#### TRF-DRAWN-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** The30HP Cart reaches0HP on a firm supported road.

**Expected result:** Its transport function is disabled and real cargo is stranded. MereHP0 does not destroy the cargo or injure every occupant.

#### TRF-DRAWN-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** The original Cart frame, main axle and bed survive; real qualified repair is available.

**Expected result:** The mundane reference uses25%of15gp=3.75gp materials,DC10 and3 active hours, unless an actual alternative complete service quote controls. No duplicate quarter-price fee.

#### TRF-DRAWN-07 — provenance

**Situation:** A20gp Player Sled and25gp Core Dungeon sled appear in the same inventory.

**Expected result:** Keep two distinct bodies and profiles. The Core item has its actual two operators,12/20Load and60lb frame, not the Player animal-drawn snow sled’s ratings.

### TRF-WATER

#### TRF-WATER-01 — admission

**Situation:** Four actual occupants including one oar/helm operator fit a Rowboat’s600lb/60Load total and400lb/40Load cargo sublimit.

**Expected result:** They can qualify only with actual geometry, at least1ft water depth, working oars/controls and a feasible route; the price supplies no fifth seat or crew.

#### TRF-WATER-02 — overload

**Situation:** A cargo-light Rowboat has five occupants.

**Expected result:** The four-person cap still fails. Low body weight does not create another seat.

#### TRF-WATER-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** A Galley’s selected oar mode lacks one of its41 required active workers.

**Expected result:** It cannot perform that staffed drive event. An actually available sail mode needs its own8 workers and usable wind; drift is only actual environmental movement.

#### TRF-WATER-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** Earlier crew Actions are paid, then the helm becomes unable before the final contribution.

**Expected result:** Do not presume a future helm Action. The event fails its current feasibility while paid contributions remain spent; a helm swap cannot yield a second event.

#### TRF-WATER-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** An afloat Rowboat reaches0HP at campaign second1000 with no overriding destruction rule.

**Expected result:** Disable propulsion and set the one flooding deadline at1600. Touching or renaming the hull does not restart it.

#### TRF-WATER-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** Two eligible workers shore that hull from1050to1350 with an actual1gp minimum patch and succeedDC15.

**Expected result:** It remains disabled afloat until4950, sixty minutes after completion. NoHP or propulsion returns. The same rupture cannot buy another shoring interval.

#### TRF-WATER-07 — provenance

**Situation:** The wreck is grounded on real support and recovered for full repair.

**Expected result:** Retain hull/primary-part IDs, actual lost cargo, costs and deadlines. Full repair needs the existing main body; a quotation cannot recreate a missing keel or lifeboat.

### TRF-HAULER

#### TRF-HAULER-01 — admission

**Situation:** The actual intact hauler has a fitting200lb/20Load payload, available oil and a qualified usable local operator.

**Expected result:** NormalSpeed30ft/3mph is possible only on a real clear firm route with the adjacent operator’s two hands and paid Action/time.

#### TRF-HAULER-02 — overload

**Situation:** A fitting emergency load is250lb/25Load; a proposed replacement is301lb.

**Expected result:** The first uses emergencySpeed5ft/0.5mph if otherwise feasible. The second exceeds the hard pound cap and cannot move routinely, even if Load fits.

#### TRF-HAULER-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** The operator is more than5ft away with no access to the controls.

**Expected result:** No commanded propulsion occurs. A class remote-binding feature is not automatically this separate hauler’s control procedure.

#### TRF-HAULER-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** Two active moving hours consume part of one pint, followed by a stationary pause.

**Expected result:** Consume exactly1/4pint for the moving interval. The pause does not consume propulsion fuel or reset that history; actual leaks remain separate.

#### TRF-HAULER-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** The hauler reaches0HP with its original primary frame, drive and controller still present.

**Expected result:** Transport stops. Its explicit reconstruction bridge needs8 active hours,DC15 and125gp actual compatible materials; no creature death saves or mundane magical salvage shortcut.

#### TRF-HAULER-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** At40 moving hours the intact mechanism receives one hour of qualified service and an actual1gp/0.5lb pack.

**Expected result:** Service clears the due-service counter only. RepairHP, fuel and lost parts remain separate, and the worker cannot claim that hour as rest.

#### TRF-HAULER-07 — provenance

**Situation:** A recipe automaton, a class device and core.transport.hauler share a descriptive name.

**Expected result:** Keep their full distinct source identities, controls, resource rules and body histories. This transport body gains noRP, attacks, class commands or harvested magical output.

### TRF-AUXILIARY

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-01 — admission

**Situation:** Two capable carriers prepare the10lb stretcher for a180lb Medium patient with20lb gear, using both hands each.

**Expected result:** The200lb patient/gear payload and6casualtyLoads fit; each normally supports105lb plus their own gear, with actual equipmentLoad shares and positions also checked.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-02 — overload

**Situation:** A stretcher is offered a301lb patient/gear payload, or a single Medium harness is proposed for an incompatible Huge puller.

**Expected result:** Neither fits its actual profile. More payment or spare unrelated Load cannot substitute the fitting stretcher or32lb Huge harness.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-03 — control-loss

**Situation:** One stretcher carrier releases the required support, or a puller’s draw harness loses its attachment.

**Expected result:** Resolve the real supported load and possible fall/stranding. The auxiliary item does not create another worker, balance or remote control.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-04 — interrupted-movement

**Situation:** One carrier Dashes while the other has only ordinary movement remaining.

**Expected result:** Coordinated motion remains bounded by the slower eligible carrier’s paid movement. No forced free Dash is assigned to the other.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-05 — damaged-or-zeroHP

**Situation:** The actual stretcher or harness body breaks under load.

**Expected result:** Resolve its real loss of support/attachment and actual landing or stranded frame. Its5HP is not an extra patientHP pool.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-06 — rescue-repair

**Situation:** A suitable replacement fitting is available but the original primary body must be preserved for repair.

**Expected result:** Use real compatible parts, qualified work and the actual repair rule; lifting/transferring the patient still takes its own support and time.

#### TRF-AUXILIARY-07 — provenance

**Situation:** The supported patient moves to another carrier after one failed harvest claim on a separate corpse.

**Expected result:** Keep each body/item and claim identity distinct. A new carrier does not change material age, revive the patient, or reopen the corpse’s terminal claim.

<a id="director-chapter-6"></a>
## 6. Reputation and background standing

### RP-A01 — Keep the original relationship record

The raw layer preserves the exact original serialized bytes, decoded value/type/presence/key, path, known version/hash, capture provenance and event history. A derived standing-v1 view is separate, with its interpretation rule, subject/institution binding and evidence. Loading, displaying, importing and exporting do not authorize normalization.

Use the named policies precisely: standing-v1; companion-score-v1-evidenced; loop-1.2-eleven-band-to-standing-v1; player-seven-band-check-modifier; qualitative-five-band-initial-grant; or unresolved. Boolean false, fractional values and numeric strings are not valid integral scores. A value already clipped by an old helper is not proven complete history.

The optional Loop mapping needs an explicit interpretation event, not an automatic migration on load and not a new general owner-confirmation gate for this authored review. Unknown evidence stays a precise unresolved relationship. No real saved characters are read or migrated here.

### RP-A02 — Preserve missing, hidden and invalid data distinctly

The new authoritative envelope contains: schemaVersion; untouched raw document bytes or a complete raw-blob reference/hash; explicit sourceFormatVersion or unknown; subjectId; capture time/provenance; ordered original entries with key/value/type/presence; actual institution bindings; interpretation events; standing events; unknown top-level/extension fields; and a disposable derivedPlayableView.

Exact bytes are necessary because an ordinary parsed JSON object can lose duplicate keys, number spelling and ordering. Preserve ordered duplicate occurrences and leave their numerical interpretation unresolved until explicitly reconciled. Parse failure retains the blob/error rather than creating an empty object.

Absence, null, zero, false, numeric1, string1, fraction1.7, invalid text, hidden-to-this-audience and unknown institution stay distinct. Visibility does not invalidate an otherwise valid private number. A setter cannot briefly admit an unknown key only for the next load to discard it.

Reject stale/replayed/conflicting writes atomically, preserving all raw data and unrelated extensions. No call to the destructive old normalize/applyBackground helper is part of this candidate. The included twelve historical probes and new preservation fixtures are synthetic evidence; deployment and migration need the later versioned implementation and backup process.

### RP-A03 — Apply background starts once

Preserve all twenty original runtime maps and full featureSource clauses internally. The clause, not an abbreviated dictionary, determines the new standing scope.

Apply these specific source completions:

- Arcane Academy Dropout: an extra academic-factions −1 is not another grant beyond the matching formal arcane institution.
- Dimensional Refugee: academic-factions 0 is not a universal additional neutral relationship.
- Seeker: religious-orders 0 is not a second group beyond the actual mainstream orders.
- Wildling Herbalist: nature-circles +1 does not duplicate an actual Druidic/wildling relationship.
- Frontier Settler: nature-circles 0 has no separate source grant; the actual frontier-trader Liked relationship must be retained even though the runtime map omitted it.
- Celestial Emissary: the religious-orders +1 scope is only the source-described qualifying orders, never every religious order.
- Relic Hunter: include both actual indigenous peoples and religious orders who regard the work as desecration; the latter was omitted from the abbreviated map.

These fresh-start dispositions do not delete existing saved keys. A runtime extra may be later earned or have another actual source. Establish the origin before replacing a contribution.

For a proven Cult error, retain the old initialization as superseded evidence, replace only its suspicious-authority contribution and replay later chronological events with their actual caps. Example: old erroneous +1 start followed by an earned +1 had shown +2; corrected −1 start plus that same earned +1 yields 0. A current +1 without history cannot be assumed to be the bad start.

### RP-A04 — A label describes a score

Never use a score-to-label-to-score roundtrip as authoritative normalization. The endpoint pairs remain distinct history states even though they share labels.

Retain invalid values and detailed errors in the private preservation record. A public unresolved message may disclose only what that audience is allowed to know. Hidden is neither absent nor null, and omission from a public projection is not deletion from the authoritative envelope.

### RP-A05 — Bind relationships to real institutions

Preserve all 44 original definitions, exact names/descriptions, nine draft flags, four resonanceTrack flags, optional/absent fields and twenty runtime background maps internally. They are source records, not automatically fully reviewed world institutions. Do not publish the full private list as a character relationship menu.

A campaign binding needs a stable namespaced ID, actual identity and jurisdiction, known members/authorized agents, actual subject relationship, references, audience permissions, supplied resources/obligations and optional qualified registry links. Missing facts remain unknown; do not fill feared secrets or doctrine from a category label.

The registry is not a closed 44-key whitelist for saved history. Unknown keys and source-specific extensions survive. An alias record identifies actual sameness and its version, while institutional splitting/merging retains distinct before/after identities and the actual political event. None of this interprets a draft faction's deep lore.

### RP-A06 — Record signed events without losing history

For each update retain proposed magnitude, scene-applied proposal, resulting score, delta from the scene opening, actual change from the prior displayed result, both overflow components, expected prior version and new version. Additional notification is not a new underlying cause.

Reject duplicate, stale or conflicting operations without partially changing other entries. Correcting a contribution requires a known replay basis; unresolved raw history cannot be turned active merely to accept an adjustment. A Boolean public-success flag is insufficient evidence of a witnessed deed, actual institution or authority.

The twelve original probe premises and their final dispositions remain complete in this module. New synthetic examples cover fresh starts, the proven Cult correction, unknown keys, zero/null/absence, duplicate keys, parse failure, +3 label roundtrip, signed opposite events and concurrent expected versions. Finite raw-byte and arithmetic checks do not authenticate a real rescue, institution, consent or campaign reputation and do not execute a production save migration.

### Worked chapter cases

#### RPX-01

**Situation:** An evidenced Loop1.2 relationship has raw+2/Favored.

**Expected result:** A recorded optional mapping derives+1/Liked while retaining raw+2, its old label and any actual earned privilege.

#### RPX-02

**Situation:** An unversioned file contains numeric+2.

**Expected result:** Its value alone does not prove companion or Loop policy. Keep it raw/unresolved with no newly derived active score.

#### RPX-03

**Situation:** An old record contains onlyHonored with established qualitative meaning.

**Expected result:** Retain its band and ambiguous numeric endpoints{+2,+3}. Do not use the fresh-start+2 mapping to rewrite this history.

#### RPX-04

**Situation:** One document contains an unknown institution key, null, false,0,"1",1.7 and an extension field.

**Expected result:** Retain every byte, key, value and type. Only the actual valid numeric value under an evidenced policy can become active; unknown identity and invalid types remain unresolved.

#### RPX-05

**Situation:** The raw JSON contains two occurrences of the same institution key with different numbers.

**Expected result:** Keep the exact bytes and both ordered occurrences; leave the conflicting active interpretation unresolved instead of accepting the last one.

#### RPX-06

**Situation:** A raw document cannot be parsed.

**Expected result:** Store the exact raw blob/hash and parse error. Do not create an empty standing map or discard extension data.

#### RPX-07

**Situation:** A fresh CultEscapee has three actually bound qualifying relationships.

**Expected result:** Create one initialization withformerCult−2,suspiciousAuthorities−1,antiCultGroups+1. Reapplying that same initialization makes no further change.

#### RPX-08

**Situation:** The old suspicious-authority initialization is proven+1, followed by a genuine later+1 scene.

**Expected result:** Append the correction of only the initialization to−1, replay the later+1 under its rules and obtain0. Preserve the old+2 result as superseded evidence.

#### RPX-09

**Situation:** A current CultEscapee score is+1 but the initialization and earned history are unavailable.

**Expected result:** Do not reset it to−1. Preserve it and flag the specific missing history/interpretation.

#### RPX-10

**Situation:** The actual sameDruidicCircle matches both a broad nature category andWildlingHerbalist’s explicit clause.

**Expected result:** Apply one real starting relationship, not two+1 grants. Keep unrelated saved keys and do not infer membership from spelling.

#### RPX-11

**Situation:** A valid current+3 is displayed asHonored and later exported.

**Expected result:** The authoritative score remains+3, with the same history. Display does not roundtrip it through fresh-grant+2.

#### RPX-12

**Situation:** One relationship is absent, oneexplicit0 and onehidden from the player.

**Expected result:** Display unrecorded versus recordedNeutral as permitted; hide the private relationship without deleting it or revealing its institutional identity.

#### RPX-13

**Situation:** Two institutions use similar names but have different jurisdiction and agents.

**Expected result:** Keep distinct stable IDs and scores. String normalization is not an alias record.

#### RPX-14

**Situation:** An unknown saved campaign key is later evidenced as a real local institution.

**Expected result:** Append its actual binding and applicable interpretation without discarding the original key or automatically assigning new standing.

#### RPX-15

**Situation:** A source faction has a draft flag and resonanceTrack:true.

**Expected result:** Preserve both flags internally. They create neither public membership nor a newRP/RC/spell rule or cosmological interpretation.

#### RPX-16

**Situation:** Opening+2 receives proposed+2, then an independent−2 in the same actual scene.

**Expected result:** Results are first+3 withscaleOverflow+1, then+2 fromsigned total0. Never calculate the second result by adding−2 to the saturated+3.

#### RPX-17

**Situation:** Opening0 has three distinct+2 ordinary events in one institution/scene.

**Expected result:** SceneProposal+6 caps at+2; result+2 andsceneOverflow+4 remain recorded without future credit.

#### RPX-18

**Situation:** Two proposed updates both expectversion7; one valid update commitsversion8 first.

**Expected result:** Reject the stale second update atomically. Preserve the input and conflict record; do not silently rebase or partially mutate unrelated scores.

#### RPX-19

**Situation:** A repeated messenger uses a different eventID but reports the same cause to the same institution/scene.

**Expected result:** Retain receipt history without another standing proposal. Duplicate underlying cause is still a duplicate claim.

#### RPX-20

**Situation:** An institution later receives evidence that its believed accusation was false.

**Expected result:** Record actual new receipt and response, with its current scene/policy. Do not silently erase the earlier belief or assume an exactly opposite numerical change.

<a id="record-contracts"></a>
## Record contracts

These specify the candidate record fields. They are not an implemented application or save adapter.

### Shared envelope

```json
{
  "id": "Stable nonblank identifier scoped to the actual physical/event identity; no renaming renewal.",
  "schemaVersion": "Exact declared contract version.",
  "sourceBindings": "Array of actual source path/hash/record/section plus reviewed-overlay IDs; not truth certification.",
  "audience": "public|director-only|restricted with explicit per-field publication rules.",
  "expectedPredecessorVersion": "Exact state version consumed by this transition; stale/missing history rejects the write.",
  "time": "{epochId, elapsedSeconds:int>=0, phase, sequence}; same authoritative campaign clock.",
  "status": "Explicit proposed|reserved|in-progress|resolved|cancelled|terminal|unresolved, narrowed by each contract.",
  "evidence": "Actual provided knowledge/authority/objects/events; reported, inferred, synthetic and verified-source arithmetic remain distinct."
}
```

### Numeric format

```json
"Every nonintegral value is an exact rational {numerator:int, denominator:positive int}; reject nonfinite/negative amounts except explicitly signed deltas/money estimates. A missing field is represented through presence, not filled withnull."
```

### EC-01 — Expedition, charter and roster

```json
{
  "id": "EC-01",
  "name": "Expedition, charter and roster",
  "authority": [
    "FL-01",
    "FL-02",
    "FL-03",
    "FL-26"
  ],
  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",
  "requiredFields": {
    "expeditionId": "Actual continuing organization identifier.",
    "charterVersion": "Prospectively accepted objective/finance/safety agreement and its predecessor.",
    "roster": "Distinct character IDs, player/NPC classification, actual class/source state and entry/exit intervals, at most12 concurrently.",
    "teams": "Combat, Reconnaissance, Research, Resource, Support; explicit actual member/time assignments, not five free staffing pools.",
    "offices": "Expedition Leader, Quartermaster, Routekeeper, Surgeon, and Claimant of Record, each bound to a real person; one person may hold multiple offices but not incompatible simultaneous work.",
    "objective": "Destination, actual success evidence and unique achievement identity.",
    "thresholds": "Prospective casualty, reserve, latest-return, gross/net/true-profit goals with units, comparator, source and evaluation times; no default promise inferred.",
    "finance": "Actual quoted/paid financing, sponsor and insurance contract IDs, real balances and liabilities.",
    "participants": "Each actual encounter/retreat/travel cohort references roster and genuine attached actors; external service providers separately classified.",
    "closure": "Real return timestamp, outcomes and unresolved obligations plus next charter; no clock, inventory or source-status reset."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "No more than12 Expedition characters; assistants and hirelings counted by actual role, not a label loophole.",
    "Suggested team sizes are recommendations. Small parties may assign several offices without receiving absent workers or bonuses.",
    "Joining/leaving does not replicate advancement, resources, prior claims or elapsed hours."
  ]
}
```

### EC-02 — Clock, assignment, work and rest

```json
{
  "id": "EC-02",
  "name": "Clock, assignment, work and rest",
  "authority": [
    "FL-03",
    "FL-24",
    "SO-06",
    "SO-11"
  ],
  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",
  "requiredFields": {
    "epochId": "One absolute campaign origin, including recorded Opening/Horn schedule receipts.",
    "interval": "[startSeconds,endSeconds), nonnegative ordered integers; all active minutes measured from actual seconds.",
    "actorId": "One real worker/creature; location and access over the interval.",
    "activityId": "Parent task and task kind, governing exact duration and native interruption/assistant rules.",
    "reservedResources": "Actual hands, Action/Reaction/regular-turn markers when applicable, tools, gear, station, receivers and persons.",
    "activeProgressSeconds": "Only genuinely performed compatible work; nominal route effort is separate from modified actual travel duration.",
    "rest": "Scheduled Rest slot fixed prospectively; actual safe rest start/end, interruptions, support, last beneficial Long Rest and Short Rest count.",
    "restDebt": "Owed flag, cause event, opening Watch, deadline Watch/time, assessed missed-rest/deadline Strain IDs and clearing event.",
    "completion": "Exactly one native check/result/receipt, or explicit incomplete/terminal outcome; no completion from a scheduling label."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "Watch=28800 seconds; day=86400; round=6. Parallel people do not multiply elapsed clock.",
    "Conflicting per-actor hands/work/control/rest reservations are rejected. Passive effects only coexist when their exact source permits.",
    "Only route-duration adjustments change a nominal travel tranche; unrelated480-minute forage/build work remains480actual minutes.",
    "Core combat endpoints and exact domain interruption rules override generic progress retention.",
    "A safe debt-clearing8-hour Rest within the reward lockout clears debt without another resource refill."
  ]
}
```

### EC-03 — Physical lots, Supply and daily support

```json
{
  "id": "EC-03",
  "name": "Physical lots, Supply and daily support",
  "authority": [
    "FL-04",
    "FL-05",
    "FL-06",
    "FL-12",
    "TR-07"
  ],
  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",
  "requiredFields": {
    "lotId": "Real original or physically traced descendant lot; parent memberships conserved.",
    "identity": "Exact source item/form/tier/properties/recipe occurrence as applicable; unknown identity is explicitly unknown.",
    "amount": "Exact rational {numerator,denominator>0} in specified physical/source units; indivisibility/precision explicit.",
    "massAndGeometry": "Actual material plus each container tare once, occupied volume/shape and allowed openings.",
    "clockAndCondition": "Original death/removal/manufacture/opening times, accumulated unprotected age, exact protection intervals, condition and disposal.",
    "custody": "Owner/title claimant, actual custodian and nested location chain without duplicate parent/child accounting.",
    "originRestrictions": "Immutable no-resource/source restrictions and existing finite harvesting/claim histories.",
    "allocation": "Recipient/day/procedure, actual committed/consumed amounts and unique consumption IDs.",
    "dailyDemand": "Piecewise actual recipient dietary coefficients over canonical day; food/water independently specified, including species-specific feed.",
    "support": "Actual required light/fuel/camp plan, paid usage, shortages and one daily Strain assessment per recipient."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "No virtual Supply inventory in addition to its real constituents. Full procedure basket and actual nutrition credits reference the same consumption, not two assets.",
    "Transfers, packing, riding, form changes, assembly and return do not reset freshness or origin.",
    "Container fit requires mass, volume, shape, closure and material compatibility independently.",
    "A food/water spell only supplies its actual selected permitted output, costs, time, lifecycle and diet contribution; no invented oil/medical supplies."
  ]
}
```

### EC-04 — Opportunity and camp

```json
{
  "id": "EC-04",
  "name": "Opportunity and camp",
  "authority": [
    "FL-06",
    "FL-07"
  ],
  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",
  "requiredFields": {
    "opportunityId": "Actual finite site/deposit/donor/forage source and predecessor depletion history.",
    "ordinaryAllocation": "Real food/water baseline, critical reserve and unavailable/forfeited matter, all disjoint.",
    "site": "Position, accessible working/sleeping area, actual environment/permissions and hazard assumptions.",
    "campTierAndMarks": "Bivouac|Temporary|Fortified|Research|Forward, exact completed Support/Research marks and source versions.",
    "materialBill": "Actual installed, stored, lost and replaced material IDs, no free new kits on a failed roll.",
    "functionalFacts": "Sleeping capacity, available tents, perimeter/watch, concealment, safe access, lights, care/work facilities, containment and current compromises.",
    "storage": "Current tier cap and actual pound/Load/volume occupancy, accessible locations for any overflow.",
    "supplyLine": "Real provider/endpoints/crew/carrier, capacity, promised and actual departure/arrival, paid charges and missed deliveries."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "Sustenance baseline0..4 is capped by actual original finite food/water; a critical adds at most ceil(S/2) real original reserve.",
    "A new Watch, worker, map or site ID does not replenish depleted material. A real new source/replenishment event needs explicit physical evidence.",
    "Tier storage replaces, not sums, previous capacity. Camp tier does not assert safe rest or create every recipe station.",
    "Camp marks and fixtures survive honestly; compromised functionality is restored only by actual necessary work/replacement."
  ]
}
```

### EC-05 — Route and travel tranche

```json
{
  "id": "EC-05",
  "name": "Route and travel tranche",
  "authority": [
    "FL-10",
    "FL-11",
    "TR-08",
    "TR-09",
    "TR-13"

  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",

    "lengthProgress": "Finite nonnegative exact route length and position→progress map; no repeated zero-length award.",
    "riskMappingQuality": "Risk0..5 where0requires actually Secured; Mapping Unknown|Sketched|Mapped|Secured; independent Core routeQuality.",
    "capacityAndAccess": "Route CapacityLoad, bridge gross weight, clearance, draft, terrain, actual closures and route-specific permission.",
    "pace": "Actual slowest required moving member/carrier, current modifiers and real current/wind; mph before and after factors.",
    "tranche": "480nominal minutes, actualSeconds after exact single quality and pace application, actor labor and pauses.",
    "mappingAndRecon": "Original marks, one applicable recon+1Progress and−1Pressure per cohort/tranche, actual evidence and completion check.",
    "forcedMarch": "Predeclared Severe task, actual roster/extra basket allocation, sole check and real spent/unspent dispositions.",
    "result": "Actual traversed path, exact credited Progress, passed obstacles, pending checkpoints and remaining time."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "Pace never overrides a full-capacity or geometric prohibition. Tactical Dash is not a perpetual overland multiplier.",
    "Hours and Progress describe one real traverse; no separate mileage entitlement and no abstract movement through blocked geometry.",
    "Route-quality/time, Burden and Strain penalties apply once each when genuinely distinct; no repeated same named penalty.",
    "Travel interruptions consume actual elapsed time without creating another full Watch or resolved-attempt retry."
  ]
}
```

### EC-06 — Pressure, complications and extraction queue

```json
{
  "id": "EC-06",
  "name": "Pressure, complications and extraction queue",
  "authority": [
    "FL-08",
    "FL-09",
    "FL-12",
    "FL-13",
    "FL-14"
  ],
  "status": "NEW explicit record contract; not an implemented adapter",
  "requiredFields": {
    "cohortId": "Actual group with parent Expedition and split/merge predecessor histories.",
    "pressure": "Integer0..6, actual current Risk floor, signed events, blocked reductions and at most2reduction credits perWatch.",
    "causeEvents": "Unique physical incident/lot/revelation/Watch IDs, distinguishing continuing and discrete causes.",
    "extractionEpisode": "Genuine return decision and destination, cargo-high-Class surcharge high-water mark, root lineage and terminal state.",
    "checkpoints": "Frozen ordered actual route positions with reason lists, processed IDs, future revisions and secondary-root references.",
    "test": "Native actor/ability/PB/assistants, tier DC and exact modifiers, actual die/roll ID, degree and declared stakes.",
    "complication": "Root causal ID, actual predeclared entry/severity/stakes, modified d6/natural face and modifier/reroll provenance.",
    "response": "Chosen actual8hdelay or finite risk, separate rescue/hazard method, costs, labor, deadlines and real losses.",
    "queue": "Finite ordered pending events at canonical time/phase/sequence, at most one Severe-linked secondary test per root."
  },
  "invariants": [
    "No old checkpoint or root event is reopened by re-entry, container transfer, a new leader or a later stronger check.",
```
